Chapter 261: Who Are You?

After Ji Yuan whispered these words to Zhang Rui, he did not turn back around. Soon, the Madam behind him had caught up, holding her fan and skirt, Wang Li following closely behind her.

“Oh, Sir, you are really strong and fast. I can barely catch up to you after just a few steps. Sir, although Miss Hongxiu is temporarily accompanying others and playing the piano, it must be almost time for her to finish up. I will make a request for you later. Please head to the boat to drink tea first, hahaha…”

Ji Yuan looked at the old woman and nodded with a smile.

“Okay, thank you for your help.”

“Ah, it’s no trouble, no trouble at all!”

The Madam put on a smile. The frequency of her fanning herself became several times faster than normal, reflecting her slightly excited mood.

Wang Li beside them followed Ji Yuan without saying a word. In hindsight, he was fairly sure the old immortal probably knew the words he had whispered to the old Madam just now.

Of course, Ji Yuan certainly was aware of what Wang Li had said. There was no need for him to guess: with his hearing, how could he not have heard it? But this situation was helpful for him, so there was no need to expose it.

The Daxiu Building was quite large, and included numerous courtyards and elegant pavilions. It could be considered the pinnacle of the brothel business. The Madam led Ji Yuan and Wang Li all the way to the back. On the way, she diligently introduced Ji Yuan to the specialties, talents, and beauty of the girls in each courtyard.

However, she could see that Ji Yuan basically never looked to the side. Even when he looked at her, it seemed casual. The madam immediately thought to herself that this gentleman was likely accustomed to beautiful appearances, and ordinary mediocre women wouldn’t catch his eye.

After a while, the group passed through the Daxiu Building and came to the water’s edge. They walked along the Sushui River on a wooden plank path by the water. In front of them was the Daxiu Ship, docked in the inner courtyard.

“Sir, Mr. Wang, the large performance boat is in front of us. The best girls in our performance pavilion are all inside! Hahaha… Please follow me aboard!”

The madam waved her fan, covering her mouth, and smiled softly. As she led the way, she carefully looked back at Ji Yuan. She could see Ji Yuan chuckling softly, before he flicked his sleeves and followed.

This flick of his sleeves actually made the Madam feel as though all the dust around her had been swept away. The bright moon in the sky was simply reflected in the water beside her.

To be honest, she had been in this business for a long time, and had seen countless men. This great gentleman’s appearance was naturally good, but there were definitely many men who were more handsome than him; however, this was the first time in her life that she had seen his calm and natural demeanor, as well as the vague traces of life’s vicissitudes on his face.

The Madam invited Wang Li and Ji Yuan to sit down In one of the elegant rooms on the boat, told others to bring tea, before taking her leave.

“Please sit here and enjoy your tea for a while. I will head to Miss Hongxiu’s attic to take a look…”

The Madam stood up and left. After exiting the elegant room at a sedate pace, she immediately sped up and hurried towards the attic where Hongxiu was.

The elegant room was quiet. Aside from the guests, everyone else, even the servants, were just waiting outside.

Ji Yuan picked up the teacup, smelled the fragrance of the tea, then drank it. Wang Li and Zhang Rui also sat on their knees to his left and right. Outside the window was a view of the calm surface of the Sushui River.

“The smell of powder on this ship is not as irritating.”

Zhang Rui said. As she held up Wang Li’s teacup to her nose and inhaled the scent, a white mist was sucked away.1 She was good at concealing herself, and with Mr. Ji’s edict, she was not afraid of her presence leaking.

Seeing this, Wang Li glanced at Ji Yuan, took a new teacup, poured himself some tea, and looked around at this elegant room in the performance boat.

The only time he had been on the Daxiu Ship was when he had been invited to tell a story. After seeing Hongxiu once, he was shocked by her beauty. After he had finished telling stories, Hongxiu had toasted him with a glass of wine, and whispered to Wang Li that he could call her “Wan’er.”2

Since then, Wang Li had become a frequent visitor of the Daxiu Building. He thought about Hongxiu day and night. Of course, the other girls in this brothel had their own talents, and Wang Li also liked them too.

“Mr. Ji…”

Wang Li said weakly, not knowing where to start.

“Mr. Wang, please tell me, I am is listening.”

Ji Yuan sipped the tea, but did not look at Wang Li. He reached into his sleeve with one hand and took out a book. In this situation, Wang Li didn’t even realize how strange it was to hide an entire book in his sleeve.

“Mr. Ji, you… you said that the current Red Beauty is… some evil thing?”

Ji Yuan flipped two pages of the book, looking for the place he had stopped at last time, and replied without looking at Wang Li.

“We’ll have to take a look at it first to see whether it’s evil or not.”

“Mr. Ji, to be honest, I am very fond of Miss Hongxiu. I met Wan’er more than a year ago… After seeing Miss Hongxiu, I always felt that this woman should not be living in this place of prostitution. If Miss Hongxiu was were really coerced by a monster, you…”

“I know, I won’t ignore it”

Ji Yuan said, and continued to flip the pages. This book was the compilation of deductions he himself had written. Sometimes he would be enlightened just by reading it again.

“Uh hehe, I…”

“Wang, are you done yet?3 What do you want to say? Mr. Ji might not be in a hurry, but I am getting annoyed!”

Zhang Rui to the side angrily interrupted Wang Li.

Wang Li smiled awkwardly.

“I believe that this might actually be a good opportunity to save Miss Hongxiu. A wonderful woman like her should be living the life of a rich lady, but she has fallen to this point…”

“Oh? Mr. Wang likes her very much?”

Ji Yuan looked at Wang Li and asked casually, the latter nodded hurriedly.

“As I just said, I have already fallen in love with her!”

Ji Yuan smiled, reached out, and lifted the teapot to refill Wang Li’s cup, then poured another cup for Zhang Rui. However, the latter only produced a small puff of white mist.

“Mr. Wang, which girls do you usually go to when you come to Daxiu Building?”

“Uh… there’s Xiaolan and Chunfang, usually… sometimes also Xiaoya…”

“Oh, I see…”

Ji Yuan looked at him.

“What is it about these girls that attracts you?”

Wang Li’s face was hot, and his speech was a little hesitant.

“It’s…that’s…they’re all very gentle…also, they’re talented…”

“Didn’t you say you fell in love with Hongxiu?”

Zhang Rui sneered from the side, and Wang Li hesitated.

“This, this is different…”

Ji Yuan sighed, shook his head, and looked at Wang Li. This was the first time tonight that Wang Li had truly seen Ji Yuan’s pale eyes clearly.

“Mr. Wang, this is not falling in love. Food and sex are parts of human nature. You are just lustful…”

“Pfft…”

Zhang Rui couldn’t help but laugh so much that she snorted tea. Wang Li was so embarrassed that he was stunned for a moment, then hurried to drink tea.

After waiting for more than a quarter of an hour, footsteps sounded from the hallway deck outside of outside the elegant room. The Madam opened the door, and her attentive voice impatiently sounded.

“Oh, I am sorry to have kept the two of you waiting for such a long time. Miss Hongxiu is retouching her makeup, she will be here soon… um…”

The Madam walked into the room and saw that there were actually three cups of tea. However, after pondering it for a while, she couldn’t come up with a reason, so she didn’t ask any more questions. She simply took a cushion and knelt beside the tea table.

“Miss Hongxiu must retouch her makeup as a courtesy before meeting new guests. I heard that a gentleman like Sir was attracted here due to her reputation. Miss Hongxiu is looking forward to meeting you. Oh, of course, we also already know about Mr. Wang’s talent…”

Ji Yuan didn’t reply. He took a tea cup, flipped it right side up, and personally poured a cup of hot tea for the Madam.

This move did not make the Madam feel as though Ji Yuan had lowered his status, but rather flattered her.

“Thank you, sir!”

Ji Yuan just nodded and continued to turn the pages of his book, but at this moment, his hand suddenly stopped moving.

“Hmm?”

“Mr. Ji, what’s wrong?”

“Sir, what’s the matter?”

Zhang Rui and the Madam asked Ji Yuan at the same time. The latter frowned and shook his head, looking at the Madam.

“Miss Hongxiu is putting on makeup?”

“Yes, yes!”

The Madam finally saw Ji Yuan’s eyes clearly at this moment, half-opened. They were a pair of pale, completely calm eyes, clear and bright, like ancient wells with no ripples.

“Do you have any advice, sir?”

The Madam asked subconsciously.

“It’s nothing!”

Ji Yuan smiled and turned the page again, and continued to read the book. Just now, that extremely unique demonic aura was rapidly fading away. It had now become so faint it was almost indistinguishable.

It must be said that Ji Yuan’s hearing, smell, and certain aspects of his vision were all abnormally outstanding. He could see the aura colors of the world, and smell the dust. This was the first time this kind of change happened in front of him at such a close distance.

Not long after, the light footsteps sounded outside again, and a particularly fresh fragrance also drifted into the room. A pair of soft white hands moved the door open, and when the door was one third of the way open, there was a slight pause.

“This little girl is Duan Muwan. I am honored to greet you two gentlemen. Mother, your daughter4 is a little late!”

The Red Beauty entered the room and bowed to everyone. She walked to the table and looked at Ji Yuan and Wang Li with gentle eyes. Zhang Rui was beside Ji Yuan, but Hongxiu appeared to be unable to see her.

“Then you all have a good chat. Shall I take my leave first?”

The Madam asked with a smile. She only walked towards the door after seeing Ji Yuan nod.

“Take care, Mother!”

Hongxiu obediently told the Madam. The latter nodded and gave her a secret wink, asking her to serve them carefully.

When the sliding door to the outside was closed again, Mis Hongxiu had refilled the cups of tea for Ji Yuan and Wang Li.

“Gentlemen, before this little girl arrived, there were only three people in total, including Mother. Why are there four cups of tea? Was this reserved for me?”

Hongxiu pointed at the vacant seat beside Ji Yuan and asked in confusion.

“As expected of Miss Hongxiu, you have excellent psychological strength.”

Ji Yuan said lightly. His pair of pale eyes met Hongxiu’s puzzled gaze. Even the hint of surprise when she saw the color of his eyes was very natural.

Ji Yuan opened his eyes wide and looked at Hongxiu calmly.

“Miss, your makeup is well done!”

Hongxiu touched her face in confusion.

“Sir, you are too kind… No matter how talented we women are, some color still can’t be hidden…”5

Ji Yuan nodded, took another cup, poured a cup of tea for the girl. The latter thanked him and drank it, covering her mouth with her sleeve.

“How did the young master of the Xiao family know you? Why did you help him replace the original with a fake?”

Hongxiu’s hand holding the cup trembled slightly. She drank the tea, put down the cup, and spoke in surprise.

“Sir, you know Young Master Xiao. He hasn’t come to see Wan’er in a long time. Although the capital is far away…”

“Miss Hongxiu, did I ever say that it was Young Master Xiao of the capital?”

Ji Yuan sneered, making the woman in front of him pause.

“It was because Hongxiu only knows this one Young Master Xiao, so I thought you were talking about him…”

“Hahahaha…”

Ji Yuan suddenly laughed.

“I once heard from a friend that there were very few people in this world who understand the art of painting skin,6 so few they are as rare as phoenix feathers and unicorn horns. To master a single path in the Way of Disguise and to excel in it is something truly miraculous, and worthy of deep contemplation. It truly is extraordinary, now that I have seen it for myself today.”

Hongxiu gripped the cup tightly, glanced at Zhang Rui casually, and then looked at Ji Yuan with a cold light in her expression.

“Who are you?!”

T/n: For those of you who may have missed it, I want to offer a layman’s translation of JY and Hongxiu’s conversation because the double-entendres are just too much fun to leave as is:

HX: “It looks like you have a 4th cup of tea, is this for me?” -> Do they know there’s a ghost in the room? (but pretending not to see it at all to act like an ordinary girl) If they let her drink it, then they don’t know that there’s a ghost. If they don’t let her drink it, they’re supernatural.

JY: “You’re mentally strong” -> complimenting her on her acting skills, basically “I know you know I know there’s a ghost, so stop pretending”

JY: “Your makeup is well done” -> You’re very good at concealing yourself, demon. You’re also very skilled in disguise techniques. And you also have an excellent excuse, you’ve integrated yourself in the human world seamlessly.

HX: “Thank you. Some things are difficult to hide, even with makeup.” -> taking his compliment literally in order to keep pretending to be a normal girl, but at the same time saying something both humble and secretly bragging about herself a little (see footnote 5)

JY: “How do you know Young Master Xiao so well for you to switch out the real Hongxiu for him?” -> abandoning all pretenses, “I know all your secrets, so seriously, stop pretending”

HX: tries to keep selling the innocent act but accidentally reveals that Xiao was from the capital so she actually does know what he’s talking about

JY: Gotcha :3 Damn, your disguise really is good.

HX: *panicking*

Reminder that when ghosts eat or drink, they consume the “essence” of the food, leaving a tasteless physical portion behind.

“er” is a diminutive indicating familiarity

The respectful way of saying “I” seems to be referring to yourself by your last name, so every time Wang Li hesitates, he keeps saying “Wang…” to refer to himself. Zhang Rui’s line here is actually “We know your last name is Wang!” to make fun of him LOL

“Mother” and “daughter” are not literal, it’s a term of address for the prostitutes and the Madam.

色相 (sèxiàng): coloration/hue/sex appeal. The sentence seems to be confusing because it’s a double-entendre. She’s saying that not even the powder can whiten her skin, being humble by saying that her skin is dark when the standard for beauty was white skin, but also subtly bragging by saying that not even makeup can get rid of her sex appeal. It’s a very subtle conversation the two of them are having.

Could refer to both makeup, but also explicitly disguise/transformation

Chapter 262: So That’s How It Happened

[T/n: note on chapter title]1

Miss Hongxiu finally stopped pretending to be ignorant as she had just now.

Ji Yuan looked at the woman’s reaction. It seemed that she didn’t recognize him. He put down the book in his hand, and answered nonchalantly.

“I remember, it was back when I was dragged by the old dragon to attend his thousand-year-old birthday banquet. After the banquet, I went back alone in a small boat. Along the way, I encountered a large official’s ship; on it were the father and son of the Xiao family.”

Everyone in the room, including Hongxiu, listened attentively to Ji Yuan’s story. Wang Li felt as though he were listening to a myth. Although Zhang Rui didn’t know too much about world of the “elite cultivators,” just by hearing about the thousand-year-old birthday banquet, she knew it was something absolutely extraordinary. Regardless of whether they were a human, monster, ghost, or god, being able to live for a thousand years meant they were definitely an incredible being.

On the other hand, Miss Hongxiu didn’t show any special expressions on her face, but in her heart she felt even more intensely nervous.

Despite the superficial appearance of a red-sleeved woman simply adding fragrance2 to a fancy room as she had just now, in truth the situation was not so calm.

What surprised Hongxiu was not only the fact that Ji Yuan had been dragged by the Old Dragon to attend his birthday banquet, but also that Ji Yuan knew about the Xiao family.

The former might have been a bluff, but the latter was likely true.

“Back then, I overheard the son of the Xiao family arguing with his father at the stern of the boat, all over a prostitute. The Hongxiu at that time should have been the true one, not you.”

At these words, Hongxiu slightly shifted her previous innocent and well-behaved appearance, and her temperament become a lot more lazy.

“Sir, how do you know that I was not Hongxiu at that time? You have never been to this performance boat before. Or could it be that you had been here and found some vulgar women to accompany you?”

After Hongxiu finished speaking, she added a teasing sentence.

Ji Yuan smiled and did not refute, but looked at her seriously and asked a question.

“Then, was it you at that time?”

The behavior of the person in front of her stunned Hongxiu for a moment. She wanted to tease and say “yes,” but looking at those calm eyes that seemed to see through her thoughts, she just frowned.

“No!”

“Ah, then it indeed wasn’t you.”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“Presumably the real Hongxiu has been hidden in a golden house3 by that Young Master Xiao.”

At the beginning, the son of the Xiao family had stated with great pride that he wanted to take the position of top scorer in the examination, but obviously he had not been very lucky — or rather, the group of candidates with outstanding talents had not been lucky, as they had run into Yin Zhaoxian, who was full of noble spirit and literary talent.

Judging from the situation of the Xiao family at the time, it is unlikely that the son of the Xiao family would actually marry Hongxiu. Not to mention, there was an imposter here, so the real Hongxiu would obviously not be allowed to be visible.

“Then, Lord Immortal, this could be considered helping lovers get married. Isn’t this a merit?”

Hongxiu, sitting in front of him, twirled a strand of hair and joked as though she did not take Ji Yuan seriously. However, in truth, from their encounter up until now, she already instinctively felt extreme danger with regards to this gentleman in front of her.

“Even if you helped them, what about you? Why did you stay on this Daxiu Ship? There are not many monsters like you in Dazhen.”

“I simply like this environment, is that so wrong? Even the God of the Sushui River doesn’t care about me. Why would you care, sir? If you were sure you could take me down, you wouldn’t be so friendly and talk so much nonsense with me.”

The verbal battle between both sides contained all kinds of temptations and insinuations.

In Ji Yuan’s eyes, waves of thin demonic energy created a strange image made of Qi around Hongxiu, in the vague shape of a white fox.

Ever since Hongxiu stopped pretending ignorance, the little bit of charm she leaked had made Wang Li to the side become dazed and stupid, just staring at her blankly. Meanwhile, Zhang Rui had already been alert and concentrated, so she had already noticed the extraordinary aspects of this monster. This made her feel even more depressed at her weakness, and she could only listen as Mr. Ji and this fox girl spoke.

Ji Yuan really couldn’t figure out the details of this fox creature, but in the end, she was only one individual.

One-on-one and at such a close distance, even the True Fire of Samadhi had a high chance of hitting the target, not to mention a slash of the Green Vine Sword. So Ji Yuan was really not afraid of this monster.

And at this moment, Hongxiu’s words just now gave Ji Yuan an opportunity to make his point.

“The God of Sushui River knows you are here, and doesn’t care about you?”

“Yes~!”

Hongxiu’s twirled her left index finger around the ends of her hair by the side of her face and leaned lazily on the table. She poured herself another cup of tea and looked at Ji Yuan out of the corner of her eyes.

“What are you going to do, summon the God of the Sushui River…”4

Just as she said this, Hongxiu happened to see Ji Yuan dip a finger into his teacup, then flick a drop with his fingers out the window.

“Plop…”

This drop of tea shot into the water through the cabin window, creating a special ripple.

“River God of the Sushui River, I request an audience!”

“Splash…”

On the water surface outside the performance boat, the tiny ripples grew bigger and bigger. In the span of just a few breaths, a huge black shadow faintly emerged from the water outside.

“Crash… Crash…”

A transparent column of water rose from the spot where the Spirit-Binding power had fallen outside the window. It twisted and flew into the elegant room, transforming into a figure made of rippling water, then quickly grew substance and turned solid.

The second half of Hongxiu’s sentence was stuck in her throat. She watched helplessly with a look of horror as a middle-aged man in a black robe appeared in the cabin.

‘No way… is this really the River God of the Sushui River? In other words… did he bind a god just now? ’

In Zhang Rui’s line of sight, she could clearly see a line of sweat oozing from the neck of the fox girl opposite her.

After taking in the current situation in the cabin, and especially after clearly noticing who was sitting at the table, the God of Sushui River was startled, and hurriedly bowed solemnly with cupped hands, folding in half until his back was level with the ground.

“Du Guangtong, the River God of the Sushui River, greets Mr. Ji!”

Although the Sushui was not that large of a river, it was still large enough to be worthy of a name. Plus, this was You Province, not too far from the Tongtian River. Thus, as Ji Yuan expected, this Water Lord knew him.

However, it was one thing to know what to expect, and quite another to answer accordingly. Thus, he asked a question, pretending to be puzzled.

“Oh? The Water Lord knows me?”

The river god lowered his hands and replied respectfully and solemnly.

“Sir, you are a close friend of the Dragon Lord. I was fortunate to have seen your face at the Dragon Lord’s birthday banquet.”

Ji Yuan looked at the river god and then at Hongxiu. The moment his eyes swept over her, he could see that her Qi was in chaos for a moment, before returning to a state of forced calm.

Ji Yuan nodded at the river god with a faint smile. However, in the next moment, Ji Yuan, who had been so friendly all night, suddenly grew stern, narrowing his eyes and shouting angrily.

“Du Guangtong, you are so bold! You dare to shelter foreign monsters and allow them to hide on the Sushui River. What is her purpose? And what is your purpose? I cannot understand it. Could it be that the Old Dragon asked you to do this?”

To have the audacity to call out the words “Old Dragon” meaningfully when they were less than a thousand miles away from the Tongtian River… Ji Yuan was perhaps the only one in the world who dared to do so.

When the River God Du Guangtong heard these words, his first reaction was panic, and his second reaction was grievance. Not long after he straightened up, he bowed once again, over and over in fear.

“Mr. Ji, I have been wronged! Mr. Ji! I definitely have never taken in any monsters from outside this country, and the Dragon Lord certainly has never issued me such an order. There are only some aquatic monsters practicing peacefully in my Sushui River. If you don’t believe me, I can summon them here immediately…”

Even Wang Li could see the panic in Du Guangtong’s expression and voice, not to mention the others. He felt as if a single word from Ji Yuan could take his life. He was so scared that he had gone beyond terrified.

Ji Yuan flipped a page of the book on the table, as if he had never stopped reading from beginning to end. The only difference since he had first arrived was that the book was now on the table, as opposed to being in his hand. At the same time, he spoke a single sentence softly, in the same indifferent tone as before.

“Miss Hongxiu, it seems that the River God of the Sushui River doesn’t know you. He doesn’t have the courage to lie to me.”

Hearing this, the River God realized that the monster in question was actually in the cabin. He turned around in anger, his eyes wide open, and stared at the woman.

Although Zhang Rui was still in the elegant room, the ghost body with a hint of divine aura definitely could not be the monster Mr. Ji mentioned. It was even more impossible for it to be Wang Li, as he could clearly see that he was a mortal. It could only be this woman, whose aura was difficult to see through.

‘It must be this damn monster that was slandering me! ’

The God of the Sushui River’s body had already filled with a faint monstrous energy and divine aura, and his expression was dark. The strong anger was almost visible to the naked eye. He did not dare get angry with Ji Yuan; he could say that he had a grudge against this woman, but since Mr. Ji did not speak, he could only stare blankly.

Hongxiu was unable to calm down at this moment. Even if she pretended, her performance would be unsteady. She did not dare to move too much, and slowly sat up straight. She was not brave enough to keep that lazy posture.

“Mr. Ji… this little girl… just made a little joke… Lord River God, please don’t blame me…”

Du Guangtong was obviously a water god, but his eyes seemed to be about to burst into fire. Even though she was not afraid of this river god, it really put a lot of pressure on Hongxiu.

“Well, since you are indeed a sentient being in this world, it is normal for you to have emotions and make jokes…”

Ji Yuan turned a page of the book again, glanced at Hongxiu, and continued.

“If Miss Hongxiu finds it interesting, you can continue to beat around the bush with me, as long as you have the patience.”

This statement was not so much a show of patience as a manifestation of strong self-confidence: no matter how hard she tried, she could never get out of this trap.

At this point, Ji Yuan seemed to have realized something. He bowed with cupped hands to the River God Du Guangtong, then stretched out his hand towards the cushion beside the table.

“I don’t blame River God Du, I can see that you have nothing to do with this girl. Please take a seat.”

Du Guangtong heaved a sigh of relief upon hearing this, and hurriedly returned the bow and sat down.

“Thank you for your insight, Mr. Ji!”

Ji Yuan saw that Hongxiu didn’t say a word, so he looked at her again and asked a simple question.

“How long have you been here in Dazhen?”

“Two years.”

“You have been hidden on this Daxiu Ship to harm people?”

Hongxiu nervously looked at Ji Yuan.

“If I said no…would you believe me…”

Chapter 263: The Sword-Blinded Fox

Hongxiu smiled stiffly once she had finished speaking, but she did not see any particular expression on his face. It was even more impossible to sense anything through those unchanging eyes.

“Since you don’t harm people and don’t wander around, it must be difficult to cultivate on this flower boat. So why did you come to Dazhen?”

Either way, Ji Yuan did not believe that this woman was just having fun. It was clear that, although this white fox had strong Qi, it also had some demonic energy in it. She was definitely not a gentle little lamb.

“Sir, I heard rumors that Dazhen has a mysterious and unpredictable hidden Immortal with vast spiritual energy and limitless power. Few people in the outside world have seen him. They only know that he seems to be a sword immortal with the power to rend the heavens…”

Hongxiu’s eyes flickered and she paused after saying these few words, then she looked at Ji Yuan again.

“Is it you, sir?”

“Hmph, Mr. Ji asked you a question, is it your turn to ask one back?”

The River God of the Sushui River snorted coldly, exerting his aura to press down on the woman beside him. Today, as long as Mr. Ji was ready to take down this monster, he would definitely be the first to do so.

Not only because the monster had slandered him before, but also because this was an excellent opportunity.

There was a well-known fact among all the aquatic creatures in Dazhen under the Dragon Lord that while Mr. Ji naturally had powerful spiritual techniques, his greatest power was actually his understanding of the “Tao.”

As long as one could establish some good karma with Mr. Ji, there was a slight chance that one would be able to receive his “immortal guidance.” He couldn’t even imagine such a big gift as the one that the Dragon Daughter received, but either way it would definitely be of great benefit to his future practice.

However, this outburst didn’t seem to elicit much reaction in the woman, who only seemed to care about Ji Yuan.

Ji Yuan gave a rare smile and looked at this Red Beauty.

“Vast spiritual energy and limitless power? Are there such practitioners in this world? I, at the very least, have never met such a capable person!”

If he was the one saying this, then it was basically half of an admission. Although his words were sarcastic, from another angle it could also be interpreted as him claiming that all the practitioners he had met were “just mediocre,” and that they were all people whose “limit” he could see at a glance.

Hongxiu took a deep breath to get rid of a slight sense of anxiety.

“Mr. Ji, I came to Dazhen just for a chance to avoid trouble, and I don’t have any intention of causing any chaos… Can you let this little girl take a look at your immortal sword?”

The River God Du Guangtong looked at the demon in amazement. His gaze seemed as though he were looking at a fool. He truly felt that this individual had no sense of self preservation: she still wanted to see the Immortal Sword, wasn’t she afraid of being struck down by that very same sword?

Ji Yuan frowned, thought for half a breath, and nodded.

The Green Vine Sword behind Ji Yuan floated in front of him and hung upright, gradually revealing its form. The simple and green, with vines and green leaves. It was agile and elegant, but did not reveal any sword intent or sword energy. It seemed almost as though it wasn’t a killing weapon, but rather a spiritual work of green art.

“Spirit nurtures the green vine, the sharp edge deeply concealed…”

Hongxiu subconsciously read the words on the scabbard, as if she could feel the endless sword intent sealed within it.

As the saying went, things would turn to the opposite when they reached an extreme. This was an Immortal Sword, after all: at this moment, there was not even a hint of its sharpness, which only made it easier to imagine what kind of unparalleled sword intent it would show once unsheathed.

The God of the Sushui River also looked at the Immortal Sword absent-mindedly.

‘This is Mr. Ji’s Green Vine Sword! ’

Hongxiu was so fascinated by the Green Vine Sword that her eyes looked a little dazed.

“What a beautiful sword…”

She subconsciously wanted to touch the Green Vine Sword, but at this moment, the Immortal Sword shook slightly.

Swish…

A white light that was barely visible to the naked eye emerged from the sword. Although this white light was extremely faint to their physical sight, it was as bright as a galaxy in their minds.

“Aaaah……”

Hongxiu screamed, and her entire body was thrown several zhang away, directly hitting the door of this elegant private room.

“Cough…cough cough cough …cough cough…”

Her hands and feet were trembling, and she continued coughing non-stop from that side of the room. The pressure on her chest only dissipated with the continuous coughing.

The Green Vine Sword was still suspended on the table, never having been unsheathed.

Just now, this sword of the mind originated from the Green Vine Sword. Ji Yuan didn’t expect this fox demon to have such a death wish to this extent, foolishly trying to touch the Green Vine Sword in her daze.

The Green Vine Sword was a Spiritual Immortal Sword. It was not a simple tool, it had its own temper. Sometimes that temper was not very good. The fox demon was in a completely defenseless state and was directly struck by the Green Vine Sword. It was enough to harm her.

“Tsk tsk tsk tsk… You deserve that!”

Zhang Rui sneered and whispered, and then looked at Wang Li’s distressed expression beside her. Suddenly, she became both angry and amused.

The God of the Sushui River also looked coldly at the fox demon, whose hands and feet were trembling. From how he was watching her coughing and not being able to stand up, that blow from before was likely not a light one, but rather a spontaneous warning from the Immortal Sword. If Mr. Ji had ordered the Immortal Sword to attack, the demon would probably have died.

Du Guangtong now felt more and more that this fox demon really had something wrong with her brain. Normal demons would bever touch an Immortal Sword with an owner like she had. No matter how kind or easy to talk to the owner of the sword appeared, now could the character of a killing weapon be gentle?

Wang Li on the side felt a little distressed for Hongxiu, but also finally began to wonder why no one else was coming in. There had been a lot of movement and noise in this elegant small room just now, but no one on the performance boat outside noticed it. After a while, he realized that the master must have cast a technique.

Hongxiu over there was injured by the immortal sword, and she had a pained expression on her face. She could feel that there were no wounds on her limbs, but rather that her mind had been injured. Now she had difficulty concentrating, and her hands and feet were slightly cramped. It took a while for it to ease up.

This kind of “headache” was extremely rare. She still had lingering fear.

Seeing that the fox demon stood up so quickly, Ji Yuan’s expression hardened. Although he didn’t show it on the surface, in his heart he was quite shocked. He almost attacked the fox demon when he saw its state a moment ago, but he hadn’t expected that she would recover so quickly.

It was truly extraordinary to recover so quickly after taking such a strong blow.

“What a beautiful sword…”1

Hongxiu cautiously approached the table, attention still on the Green Vine Sword, which had returned to floating behind Ji Yuan. Although the Immortal Sword had faded from sight, she could still see its afterimage from the strike on her mind.

“If only I had an Immortal Sword…”

Hearing Hongxiu’s sigh, now even Ji Yuan believed that this fox demon truly had something wrong with her brain.

Under Ji Yuan’s frowning and focused gaze, Hongxiu came to her senses and remembered the current situation. She spoke nervously with some embarrassment.

“Mr. Ji, my name is Tu Siyan, and I come from the Jade Fox Sacred Cavern in Qiancang Mountain, Lan Province, in the Western Regions. I have also met other immortals. I really haven’t done anything that violates the Tao… at least not since I came to Dazhen!”

“Lan Province in the Western Regions? Tu Siyan? Not Tushan Siyan?”2

It was a fox demon and its surname was Tu, so Ji Yuan asked this subconsciously.

“Huh?”

Tu Siyan was stunned for a moment, not understanding why the person in front of her asked this question.

Ji Yuan didn’t respond again. He closed the book on the table and put it in his sleeve. After thinking for a while, he looked at Tu Siyan.

“Miss Hongxiu, let’s have a calm chat. First, I have several doubts, and second, I do not wish to scare the people on this ship. However, you do not need to be a prostitute on this performance boat. Find an excuse to purchase yourself and leave Dazhen.”

“Oh, huh!?”

Tu Siyan responded first, then suddenly realized something and shook her head hurriedly.

“No, no, no, I can’t go out! Unless you lend me your Immortal Sword, sir. I will return it to you after a trip back to Lan Province in the Western Regions!”

Before Ji Yuan could say anything, Zhang Rui and the River God Du Guangtong were so angry that they stood up immediately.

“Demon, do you really want to court death? Mr. Ji, if this is how it will be, then let me do it. There is no need for you to take action. I will take her down for you!”

Tu Siyan leaned a little further away from Du Guangtong, clasped her hands together, and looked at Ji Yuan — or the Immortal Sword behind him — with a distressed expression. Either way, the River God did not dare to make a move, until he heard Ji Yuan say something lightly.

“Then I will have to trouble the River God to do so.”

Du Guangtong and Tu Siyan were both stunned for a moment, then the former was immediately overjoyed, while the latter was horrified.

“Roar…”

The River God let out a low roar. Divine light lit up all over his body. The hand in his sleeve was suddenly covered in a layer of black scales, and shot out like lightning, grabbing the fox demon’s neck.

Tu Siyan wanted to jump away and dodge, but the moment Ji Yuan opened his mouth, she suddenly found that her body was not obeying her commands, and that she couldn’t move at all.

“Ugh…”

Tu Siyan, whose neck was locked, struggled for a while. Then she felt that the control of her body was slowly restored, but the divine light and monstrous energy in the black hand that was strangling her neck was extremely dangerous, so she did not dare to resist too much.

Du Guangtong was also very surprised that this monster was so easy to catch.

Then the two of them seemed to realize something. One leaned forward as the other turned her neck with difficulty, looking at Ji Yuan, and found a pool of water on the table, which had gathered in a small character: the word “freeze.”

Ji Yuan did not have much confidence in using this immobilization method on this fox demon, but it was different as her mind had been injured just now, and it had truly trapped her for a moment.

Chapter 264: The Inner World Laws At the Level of Heaven and Earth

However, as Du Guangtong was strangling the fox demon’s neck, he gradually felt that something was wrong. He had clearly used his spiritual energy to hold the thin white neck in place, but he felt a strange feeling of powerlessness.

Under Du Guangtong’s tremendous strength, the fox demon seemed to be in extreme pain, and her face above the neck was flushed red. She grabbed Du Guangtong’s arms with both hands to try to pry them apart, and her feet twitched from time to time.

Ji Yuan frowned and stared at Tu Siyan, who was struggling like an ordinary person. A sense of incongruity lingered. He looked at Du Guangtong, he found that the latter was also solemn. There seemed to be the sound of a gas leakage, and a foul stench filled in the room in an instant.

Du Guangtong stared at the painted skin in his hand, and then looked at the corpse on the ground.

On the painted skin was the face of a beautiful woman, the previous Hongxiu. On the ground was a rotting female corpse. They had no idea how long she had been dead, but the stench was already overwhelming.

“Ugh… ugh…”

Wang Li, who had just been frightened by the “murder,” could not help but vomit again after seeing the situation clearly. He threw up all the food that he had eaten this evening and hadn’t digested yet. After vomiting, he ran to the window to get some fresh air, not daring to stay by the table.

Ji Yuan rubbed his temples and stood up slowly.

He opened his supernatural eyes as wide as he could. At the same time, he circulated a large amount of spiritual energy to his eyes, and visualized his inner world, but did not manifest it. Instead, he deliberately spread it out, sweeping it over the corpse and painted skin in Du Guangtong’s hand, and swept on to other locations.

When Ji Yuan visualized his inner world, Du Guangtong vaguely felt that, although Mr. Ji was still standing in front of the table, his figure seemed to stretch infinitely between heaven and earth, as if he had turned into a giant, standing amidst the mountains and rivers, with a pair of eyes like the sun, moon, and stars illuminating the world.

After about two breaths, Ji Yuan looked to the east.

“Tu Siyan, I don’t know if it’s her real name or a pseudonym…”

Ji Yuan muttered to himself. Feeling that his mental energy had been depleted too quickly, he returned to normal, and let out a shallow breath.

“Haah…”

Ji Yuan simply did this all subconsciously. In truth, this did not actually extend his vision, but rather gave him a vague sense of direction. However, this illusionary sense was very faint, and short-ranged.

Far to the east, a thin layer of smoke rolled and stretched from time to time, occasionally forming shape of a fox. However, at this moment, the cloud seemed to jump suddenly, turning back into the fox form, and turned to look west.

The feeling just now suddenly made the fox’s heart leap. As he turned his head, saw the illusion of a giant standing between heaven and earth, wearing a green robe, arms behind his back. His eyes looked at the fox indifferently.

At this moment, the fox demon’s heart nearly stopped, but the next moment, this feeling disappeared, as if it was just an illusion.

‘Ji Yuan… he is truly incredibly. It seems that the really is a master at the level of a True Immortal. This time I played with him a bit, and it doesn’t leave me with a good feeling… Oh! The Green Vine Sword is really beautiful, but what a pity that my idea didn’t work… In addition to the supernatural ability to immobilize people, what other technique did he use just now? ’

. . .

On the big performance boat, Ji Yuan realized that he had been fooled. He also realized that, although he had raised his evaluation of the fox demon on the ship several times, he still obviously underestimated her. He didn’t know whether it was lucky or unfortunate that she had escaped.

After thinking about it for a long time, Ji Yuan laughed at himself.

“Haha… I relied on my own meagre skill to underestimate the warriors of the world…”

Meagre skill?

Du Guangtong subconsciously looked down at his chest and abdomen. Then what am I? A small loach in the water?

Although the end result proved that the demon was indeed skilled, and actually slipped away from Mr. Ji’s eyes, Du Guangtong didn’t dare to laugh at him.

Mr. Ji’s method of restraining the soul was powerful, but it was not too outrageous when one thought about about it carefully, but Du Guangtong had never seen or heard of that kind of immobilizing technique. Then there was that feeling that appeared for a moment. That feeling was incredible and indescribable for the River God Du Guangtong.

‘Is this the ability level of a True Immortal? Can the Dragon Lord also do this…’

Du Guangtong couldn’t help but think this in his heart, but he vaguely felt that not all experts at the True Immortal level could accomplish this. Then, his thoughts changed, and he felt a sense of fear.

‘To be able to escape from Mr. Ji’s, that demon must be terrifyingly powerful. If it wasn’t for Mr. Ji, I would have had to face her alone one day…’

Ji Yuan didn’t know that the River God was going through so many twists and turns in his mind. Instead, he was rather embarrassed to have been slapped in the face. However, there were more troublesome things waiting to be solved in the moment.

“Ugh… haah… haah… bleeeegh… Ji, Mr. Ji, what should I do with this corpse? Hongxiu, Miss Hongxiu is gone?”

Wang Li, who was still vomiting a lot, was still concerned about Hongxiu. In a depression and a panic, he asked a question.

“Indeed, is this female corpse truly Hongxiu?”

Zhang Rui jumped over the table and walked over to the female corpse to take a closer look. However, she obviously couldn’t tell who this person was.

Ji Yuan calculated a divination with his fingers and shook his head.

“Hongxiu isn’t dead yet. This female corpse came from a mass grave outside the city. However, the Hongxiu that was here tonight is certainly dead…”

Ji Yuan was a little worried.

“The Madam will come to look for her soon.”

Wang Li suddenly became more energetic, and spoke in a panic. Suddenly, he realized that there were gods and immortals present, and immediately felt a lot more at ease.

“We didn’t kill anyone!”

Zhang Rui replied angrily, then looked at the River God who had been standing respectfully beside Ji Yuan in awe, and then looked at Ji Yuan again.

“Sir, what should we do about Hongxiu? Where is the real Hongxiu?”

Ji Yuan smiled at her helplessly.

“Send the body back to its original resting place. As for the true Hongxiu, we have to go find Young Master Xiao. As for the Hongxiu that was here tonight… naturally, we must purchase her freedom. Miss Zhang Rui, you have to work hard. “

“Huh? Purchase her freedom? I have to work hard?”

Zhang Rui initially thought that Mr. Ji was asking her to move the body, but then she found that Mr. Ji had taken the painted skin from the River God, and felt that something was wrong.

Some time later, the cabin had almost been restored to the state it had been in before the Madam left. Hongxiu, Ji Yuan, and Wang Li were all present. However, now an additional person, the River God Du Guangtong, was present.

Some stains in the room had been cleaned up using spiritual techniques and restored to neatness.

Zhang Rui was extremely unaccustomed to her current appearance. Taking clothes from a corpse didn’t matter to her, but asking her to pretend to be a prostitute…

To be honest, even if she had been dead for many years, even if she had become a goddess, she was still a woman nurtured in this social environment, and she naturally felt uncomfortable with the current situation.

Of course, this was all tolerable, but the only thing that was unbearable was the look in Wang Li’s eyes, which made Zhang Rui want to tear this guy apart, and want to gouge out his eyes even more than she had before.

But Wang Li wasn’t really watching because of lust. Of course, lust was a small factor, but it was mostly curiosity, seeing Zhang Rui’s transformation into the “Red Beauty” like a living person.

‘This is the best technique of the gods! ’

Was Wang Li’s current thought.

“What question?”

Zhang Rui hoped to get this over with as soon as possible. Du Guangtong and Wang Li also looked at Ji Yuan and waited for his next words.

Ji Yuan smiled a little awkwardly, and took out some pieces of gold and silver from his sleeve.

“I have spent over 100 taels of silver in the past ten years. This is all I have now, maybe about 20 or 30 taels of silver… Hah… Tell me, how much do you think it would cost to ransom the famous Red Beauty of You Province, whose name is known far and wide?”

“Uh… one thousand taels?”

Zhang Rui said carefully, and Wang Li immediately shook his head.

“It’s definitely not enough, not even a fraction of the amount!”

Ji Yuan felt that their biggest hope among this group of people was Du Guangtong. In his previous life, the River Gods and Dragon Lords on TV had the most treasures.

“River God Du, I wonder how much silver you have?”

Ji Yuan asked shamelessly. Du Guangtong scratched his head.

“Mr. Ji, I have never had any gold or silver… What use are these objects to me?”

It was over, their best hope was gone…

“Knock knock knock…”

At the moment the Madam knocked on the door outside, Ji Yuan stopped his isolation technique, connecting the inside and outside of the room back together.

“Sir, it’s almost dawn, and your room has been arranged, why don’t you go and rest now?”

The Madam walked in with a smile, shaking her fan.

“Daughter, do you want to keep this Mr. Ji tonight? And these two…”

‘Hmm? Two?’

The old lady wiped her eyes and looked again. Only Wang Li and Ji Yuan were present. Just now, she seemed to have seen a man in black clothes. It seemed she was dazed due to staying up late.

“Oh, and Mr. Wang, Xiaoya is waiting for you!”

Zhang Rui’s expression was extremely unnatural, and she smiled stiffly, her hands under the table already clenched into fists.

“No, no need, Mother, Mother.”

“Huh?”

The Madam was stunned for a moment. Why did this Hongxiu become dazed again today?

“Madam, how much silver is needed to ransom Miss Hongxiu?”

Ji Yuan asked. Either way, he didn’t plan to use real gold and silver, just a spiritual technique to turn things into silver to bluff.

Chapter 265: The Fierce “Red Beauty”

“Huh?”

The Madam looked at Hongxiu, who was actually Zhang Rui in disguise, and felt as though she had guessed some of the reasons why she currently seemed unnatural.

Seeing the Madam chattering non-stop, and her conditions and probing, Ji Yuan interrupted her immediately.

Wang Li immediately shut up. He had been thinking superficially, but now he remembered that the other party was not an ordinary mortal at all.

Outside, the prostitute dragged Hongxiu along the corridor and all the way into another elegant room, then closed the door before speaking.

The Madam narrowed her eyes and said what she thought was a heartbreaking sentence.

The Madam remembered clearly that it was at that time that her daughter finally let go of her last layer of restraint.

However, she failed to see the expression she expected on Hongxiu’s face.

“Hmph, you may still get some money if you speak frankly, otherwise you will get nothing!”

Zhang Rui didn’t have the patience to speak slowly. If this didn’t work, she could always fight her way out.3 There were many women who fled from the brothels.

She settled on a half-truth.

The two muscular women, who weighed nearly two times as much as Hongxiu, stagged backwards as they were slapped. They took seven or eight small steps back but were still unable to stabilize their bodies in the end.

“Ugh…”

Zhang Rui snorted coldly and left with a flick of her sleeves.

This scene shocked the Madam who watched Hongxiu leave but didn’t dare to stop her.

After a while, Zhang Rui returned to Ji Yuan’s elegant room, opened the door, and said.

“Mr. Ji, I’ve thought about it. Do we really need to care about respecting these mortals? At worst, we can just fight our way out. Either way, it will work as long as the end result is proof of Hongxiu leaving the brothel.”

In fact, it was also viable to allow Ji Yuan and the others to leave first, and Zhang Rui to escape later, but she really didn’t want to stay here for even one more moment.

Ji Yuan bowed to Zhang Rui with cupped hands in apology.

“Miss Zhang, don’t worry. Your disturbance just now worked perfectly. It’s always better not to make a big fuss when there is an option to get out safely. To put it bluntly, from the perspective of the Madam, all the women here are commodities to be sold to some extent. If the price is right, it will be easy to negotiate.”

“What if she really dares to ask for a sky-high price?”

Wang Li asked subconsciously.

“We’ll pay for it by playing some tricks. Someone will pay it later… And that someone will be that man!”

Ji Yuan thought for a while and finally spoke. Then he turned around and bowed to the River God Du Guangtong.

“River God Du, this time it’s up to you.”

Du Guangtong drank the tea in the cup in front of him, stood up, and returned the bow.

“Don’t worry, Mr. Ji. I will definitely get it done. I will take my leave first!”

“Okay, River God, take care!”

Du Guangtong then bowed slightly to Zhang Rui and Wang Li before turning into a stream of water and light, sailing out the window without waiting for the two to return the bow.

“Mr. Ji, what is the Lord River God going to do?”

Zhang Rui had just come back and didn’t know what they had been discussing before, so she was very curious. Wang Li coughed and spoke in a storyteller’s tone.

“As soon as Lord River God left, he will have gone to lead the good monsters under his jurisdiction to come ashore and disguise themselves as a team of people, bringing the ransom for Miss Hongxiu.”

They still had to keep up appearances. It was definitely not suitable for someone like Ji Yuan to take out so much wealth.

That night, there were too many things happening in Daxiu Building.

A very noble and mysterious guest came, took a fancy to Hongxiu at first sight, and wanted to ransom her.

Then came a large group of ferocious servants. Just being caught in their gaze caused sweat to beat on the backs of the more muscular servants in the Daxiu Building.

However, the fiercest one was not these outsiders, but Miss Hongxiu herself. This girl, who was proficient in music, chess, calligraphy, and painting, was so fierce that night that she suppressed all the women in the Daxiu Building. More than one person sensed that the Madam was a little afraid of her.

Of course, in the end, Daxiu Building did not suffer any loss, and the wealth they obtained made the Madam smile reluctantly. Although her golden goose that laid golden eggs was gone, she still got a lot of money.

But this was all a slap in her face by her “daughter,” which pained the Madam even as she smiled.

A small box of gold, a small box of heavy pearls, and five hundred taels of gold was certainly no small amount, but the pearls were particularly precious. Each one was big and round, a type that was very difficult to buy with just money. It was enough to drive most women crazy.

The Daxiu Ship of the Daxiu Building also contained many women of good repute. They were not supported by Hongxiu alone. The loss of Hongxiu would not hurt them; they had already formed a well-practiced training model, and could immediately vigorously raise a newcomer.

In the daytime the day after, a small boat floated down the Sushui River, dozens of li outside the capital of Chengsu Prefecture. On this boat were Ji Yuan, Du Guangtong, Zhang Rui, and Wang Li.

This boat was a bit like the one that Ji Yuan had taken on the Chunmu River back then. It could easily hold seven or eight people. The one rowing the boat was Ji Yuan.

“We ‘passed off fish eyes for pearls,’4 the saying goes. I finally experienced it for myself last night.”

Ji Yuan smiled and chatted with Du Guangtong, who was standing beside him. The River God had been very meticulous. Ji Yuan hadn’t needed to do much afterwards.

“Hey, Mr. Ji, you are too kind. I went to find someone from the underworld of Chengsu Prefecture and checked the ransom prices of several courtesans from Daxiu Building in recent years. A few hundred taels of gold is already a sky-high price. If the individual were older or less good-looking, it would be even less. With the gold we gave, Daxiu Building won’t lose money.”

The gold they had given was all real, but the pearls were fake.

Du Guangtong had “borrowed” a box of gold from the vault of a large bank in Chengsu Prefecture. Ji Yuan would find the person who had hidden away the real Hongxiu to make up for it.

But Daxiu Building did not deserve that box of pearls in the first place. Du Guangtong would personally go “retrieve” it in a few days.

Du Guangtong hopes that the person they were going to find could not come up with the money in the end, that way he could help Mr. Ji solve his problem by finding a way to properly resolve this matter, and make the good relationship between him and Mr. Ji even more stable.

Although the Sushui River was not directly connected to the Tongtian River, there were several small canals for freight transfers that connected them. Ji Yuan was not impatient, so he planned to row to the Capital Prefecture. With his rowing speed, he could reach the capital in less than half a month.

After chatting a bit more with the River God, the latter finally bid farewell and left. Ji Yuan looked at Wang Li, who was dozing in the cabin, thought for a while, and said.

“Mr. Wang, you didn’t sleep at all last night, please rest first. When you wake up, I will have a little story to tell you, or you can compile it in a formal book.”

Wang Li let out a dazed “hmm,” leaned back in the cabin, and continued dozing off. He soon fell asleep.

Chapter 266: May You Always Be Like This

Almost ten days later, the boat carrying Ji Yuan, Wang Li, and Zhang Rui was sailing down a small river, gradually approaching the Tongtian River.

The boat sailed smoothly and hardly shook. On the small table in the cabin were brushes, paper, and an inkstone. Wang Li was writing something on the table, while Zhang Rui sat next to him, watching.

After a long time, Wang Li finally wrote the last word, heaved a sigh of relief, and put the brush on the brush stand.

Looking at Zhang Rui next to him, Wang Li remembered his previous mistake and quickly asked Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji, with regards to the people in your story, do you mind if I adapt some of the plots appropriately? Some things are still very inconvenient to write into the book. It would be more appropriate to change it to the previous dynasty, or make up a dynasty.”

Ji Yuan had just finished telling the story of the Old Turtle of the Chunmu River. Since this was Mr. Ji’s story, it most likely all actually happened.

No matter how ignorant Wang Li was, he knew that this kind of story could not be published without modification.

The things he experienced during this period were quite a shock to Wang Li’s heart, but when it came to his work as a storyteller, he could still maintain a relatively calm mind.

“Hahahaha… As long as you don’t change it too much, the old turtle won’t mind.”

“Hmm…”

Wang Li responded. He thought for a while, but hesitantly continued to speak.

“Mr. Ji, I actually think the ending of this story is a bit inappropriate.”

Ji Yuan gave a stroke of the oar and replied casually.

“Mr. Wang, what do you think is wrong? Tell me.”

By now, Wang Li had roughly understood Ji Yuan’s temper, so he spoke with confidence.

“Mr. Ji, this story has twists and turns. It has miracles, the vicissitudes of life, and a lot of depth, but I still feel that this ending is not quite right. Think about, it whether its the emperor of this dynasty or the Xiao family, they may have to bear some repercussions. You are an god, and naturally understand the outcome, but it’s not so obvious to me.”

Wang Li considered his words and continued.

“I believes that storytelling, in addition to bringing a little joy to the public, also plays a role in alerting the world. It is indeed difficult to balance the retribution of good and evil in this world, but at least in the a story, it can and should be done. If not even a storyteller’s tale can allow people to happily overcome evil, how boring would that be…”

“Clap clap clap clap…”

Ji Yuan put down the oar and applauded Wang Li.

“Well said, well said! Change it as your heart desires.”

“Haha, then I will… just change it?”

Wang Li asked again to confirm. Ji Yuan rowed again, nodding and emphasizing.

“Change it!”

That being the case, Wang Li was no longer polite. He took the pen, dipped it in ink, turned to a blank page and began to write again.

What he had now was only the first draft. He had simply written down what Mr. Ji said before his memory faded. He would then refine it and gradually perfect the story.

To form a good story that could be deeply remembered, the core plot was of course indispensable, but the storyteller’s polish was also crucial. Wang Li had worked hard on this process. After all, compared to relating some rumors he had heard or copying other famous stories, this kind of true supernatural story could make him even more excited.

Zhang Rui looked at Wang Li with some surprise. This man was like an entirely different person from who he had been in the brothel when he was writing and telling stories. This question and answer exchange with Mr. Ji just now surprised her even more.

Just due to the fact Wang Li was able to express what he had just now, which evoked Mr. Ji’s applause, Zhang Rui looked at Wang Li with new eyes.

Thinking carefully, the original adaptation of “The White Deer’s Fate” actually had a similar impact. She felt disgusted at hearing it only because she happened to be the “victim” of the adaptation. And although Bai Lu and Zhou Lang’s ending had a tragic core, in the end they were reunited in the underworld, and were still a couple. This deepened the tragedy even further, but the strictness of the City God’s law was also impressive.

However, it was just that Wang Li didn’t know how heavy the whipping of the underworld was at the time, so the story was over-interpreted by those who had some true knowledge.

Wang Li was still scribbling and revising, and would stop from time to time to grind ink. Apart from occasionally looking at what he had written, Zhang Rui spent most her time with her eyes closed to cultivate. She could not access incense power and faith for the time being, but she could slowly absorb the Yin energy that came up from the water.

About half a day after the boat followed the small stream and merged into the Tongtian River, some odd bubbles began to appear beside the boat.

“Mr. Ji?”

Zhang Rui opened her eyes and asked after discovering the abnormality, but found that Ji Yuan just shook his head.

“Not a problem.”

Sure enough, everything returned to its original state after a while, and nothing strange happened in the water. Wang Lize had no idea that Zhang Rui had been on guard, and was still immersed in his own story world.

When they arrived at the Tongtian River, there were more hills and woodlands along the bank. About a quarter of an hour later, Ji Yuan maneuvered the boat around a winding curve, and Zhang Rui suddenly found two people standing on the shore near the hill.

The two were dressed in gorgeous clothes. One was a man, and the other was a woman, and they were both very beautiful. Even Hongxiu’s face was eclipsed by the woman’s appearance. But how could there be two people standing on the shore in such a remote and desolate place, with no carriages or boats nearby?

“Hey, Wang Li, there’s a beautiful girl over there.”

“Oh.”

Wang Li responded without looking up. After writing a few words, he seemed to come back to his senses. He hurriedly looked in the direction Zhang Rui was pointing and saw those two people with outstanding appearances.

Zhang Rui and Wang Li then realized that, in addition to the two people who had been looking at the boat, the boat was also approaching the shore.

“Ying Feng…”

“Ying Ruoli…”

“… greets Uncle Ji!”

The Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter solemnly bowed to greet them when the boat was still seven or eight zhang away.

Ji Yuan put down the oars at the bow of the boat and bowed in return.

“Hello, my two nephews!”

Normally, Ji Yuan would address them as “His Royal Highness Ying” and “Lady River Goddess.”

But Wang Li was currently on the boat now, and if he called them that directly, he would probably be shocked again, so he simply called them “nephews” this time.

In fact, after seeing Wang Li’s reaction to the shock he received a few days ago, Ji Yuan didn’t want Wang Li to be exposed to too many supernatural incidents. After he had finished telling this story, it would be better for him to be a pure storyteller.

The boat had just docked, and the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter stepped on the bow together, then the boat continued to sail along the river towards the capital.

Ever since the two of them had gotten on the boat, although there was no power and divine light, Zhang Rui subconsciously didn’t dare to speak. Even Wang Li only dared to take a peek at the two of them, mainly writing with his brush.

“Uncle Ji, are you going to the capital? My father is sleeping, do you want to wake him up?”

Judging from Ji Yuan’s posture, he definitely didn’t come to the Tongtian River in particular, and he still had some respect for the Old Dragon.

“No, it’s fine.”

“Uncle Ji, who are these two?”

The Dragon Daughter walked to front of the cabin, bowed to the two people inside, and asked.

Zhang Rui and Wang Li quickly returned the greeting.

“My name is Zhang Rui, I’m from… Yan Province. His name is Wang Li, a storyteller.”

Wang Li looked at Zhang Rui and twitched his mouth.

“Uh, I’m Wang Li, a storyteller.”

“Well, I am Ying Ruoli, that one is my elder brother Ying Feng. Both of us are Mr. Ji’s juniors.”

After Ying Ruoli entered the cabin to find out what Wang Li was doing, she quickly went out to chat with Ji Yuan.

The two extra people on the boat stayed for half a day. Ji Yuan did not dock the boat until the next pier.

Zhang Rui and Wang Li knew it was time to part, and the two of them went ashore, while Ji Yuan and the other two stayed on the boat.

“If you take a boat from this dock and go southeast along the Tongtian River, you can quickly reach Yan Province.”

They had agreed on this long ago: Zhang Rui would naturally not go to the capital. Wang Li wanted to complete his story and follow these gods, but Ji Yuan did not intend to bring him along all the time. He had taken him away from Chengsu Prefecture because he had been afraid that he would be liquidated by the brothel. He also wanted to keep him away from that land of flowers.1

At this time, Zhang Rui had already vanished from sight and stood by Wang Li’s side, bowing and saying goodbye to the people on the boat. Wang Li was even more excited.

“Mr. Ji, when will I see you again? Do you think I have a chance to… to become a person like you…”

Wang Li on the shore looked at the people around the docks and asked a veiled and expectant question, which made Zhang Rui, the Dragon Son, and Dragon Daughter laugh.

Ji Yuan understood what Wang Li meant. Everyone wanted to be a god, and indeed, it was quite a good position.

“Hahahaha… Mr. Wang, be a good storyteller, and we can meet again.”

Ji Yuan smiled and used the bamboo pole on the boat to push off of the dock and into the river. Wang Li watched the boat sail away from the shore, feeling a little lost.

When the boat was more than ten zhang away from the dock, Ji Yuan suddenly turned around and shouted.

“Mr. Wang, what you said on the boat the other day, will your feelings always stay the same?”

Ji Yuan asked this question without any reason. The Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter didn’t understand, and even Zhang Rui didn’t respond for a while, but Wang Li immediately understood what Mr. Ji was asking in an instant. His mind flashed back to what he had said the day before — that he wanted to change the ending of the story, and Mr. Ji’s applause at that time.

“Mr. Ji! I will always be like this!”

Wang Li shouted his response to the river.

Ji Yuan nodded and bowed solemnly towards the shore again.

Seeing this, Wang Li did not dare to be negligent, and hurriedly returned the greeting. Then he watched the boat go farther and farther, before it soon became blurred in his sight.

“Let’s go, Mr. Wang, don’t even think about sneaking to the capital!”

“Hey, Miss Zhang, you… Okay, okay… But don’t you think Miss Ying’s name sounds familiar?”

“Familiar? You must be out of your mind!”

“I’m not really…”

Wang Li gave a wry smile and went to find a boat. From an outsider’s perspective, this guy might indeed be a little out of his mind for talking to himself.

On the Tongtian River, Ji Yuan rowed the oar with one hand, and his right hand extended into a sword finger position. An illusory chess piece flashed.

“May you always be like this…”

“Uncle Ji?”

“It’s nothing.”

Ji Yuan rowed the boat, this time much faster than before.
