i.e. brothel

Chapter 267: Debt Collector

After the two outsiders left, the Dragon Son and Daughter discussed somethings without reservation.

As the River Goddess of the Tongtian River, the Dragon Daughter also had responsibilities. In general, no one would dare to disturb Ji Yuan when he practiced in his own home. Since he had come out this time, she naturally had to report some events.

“Uncle Ji, We have mobilized a small number of aquatic creatures from the sea, and gone into some swamps along the waterways by the sea in Eastern Cloud Continent to investigate.”

“What did you find?”

Ji Yuan asked. The Dragon Son answered first.

“Most aquatic creatures don’t care much about the things on shore. They had no unusual situations to report for the time being. However, a local river god told us that the waterway had been blocked by corpses for ten days, and tens of thousands of people died.”

Ji Yuan was startled and turned to the Dragon Son.

“What happened?”

“It’s not evil spirits, but rather a military confrontation. The two sides fought fiercely by the river. One side attacked halfway across the river, and the defeated side suffered heavy casualties.”

Ji Yuan frowned. There were always wars in this world. Even Dazhen had fought one of the neighboring countries a few years ago. There were almost always constant small frictions at the border, especially in a land the size of Eastern Cloud Continent.

“Is it the country of Tianbao?”

“No, it’s in a country called Daegu.”

Ji Yuan said “oh,” and didn’t say anything more. He just rowed his oars. Although the rowing frequency was not that high, the force of each stroke seemed to sweep away the waters behind the boat. The strokes appeared to be powerful and heavy, so the boat sailed along at high speeds. This process did not use internal energy to mobilize the surrounding spiritual energy, but instead was simply strength and technique.

The boat was quiet for a while. The Dragon Daughter sat on the wooden bench in the cabin, her hands on her knees and her eyes closed, while the Dragon Son sat casually on the side of the boat at the stern.

“Don’t you have your own things to do?”

Seeing that they seemed to want to keep following him, Ji Yuan couldn’t help but ask.

“Hey, I really don’t have anything to do. My sister is a river god, she has a lot of things to do.”

Hearing what her brother said, Ying Ruoli couldn’t help but quickly defend herself.

“I’m fine too. A river god is not like a city god, how can I be in charge of things all day long?”

“Uncle Ji, what are you going to do in the Capital Prefecture this time? The entire Capital Prefecture is now empty. There aren’t even lonely ghosts, let along evil spirits.”

Ji Yuan looked at Ying Feng and told the truth.

“Two reasons: one is to collect debts, and the other is that the old emperor of Dazhen is about to die. Let’s see how the dynasty of Dazhen will determine its own destiny.”

“Collecting debts?”

The Dragon Son and Daughter were curious. Was there anyone in the Capital Prefecture who would dare owe Uncle Ji a debt?

“Uncle Ji, who owes you a debt? My father is asleep. If you want to collect on the jar of Ambergris that he lost to you in the last bet, you should go under the water. He doesn’t intend to default on it…”

“Of course it’s not Mr. Ying, its a mortal.”

Ji Yuan smiled and explained.

The Dragon Children were even more curious. A mortal who was strong enough to owe Uncle Ji a debt? No matter what he owed, they must take a look at this person.

. . .

Two days later, in the evening, the setting sun had already fallen halfway down the horizon. Ji Yuan took the Dragon Son and Daughter to walk along Xining Avenue of the Capital Prefecture.

In order to prevent the Dragon Daughter’s appearance from causing trouble, the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter concealed themselves. They were now only just above average in both clothing and appearance.

“Uncle Ji, I heard that you have a registered disciple in the underworld of the Capital Prefecture?”

The Dragon Son Ying Feng suddenly asked, causing Ji Yuan, who was walking in front, to pause and turn his head to look at him. When he saw the Dragon Son grinning, Ying Ruoli looked at him intently. Obviously she too wanted to know.

“Where did you learn about this?”

Even the white deer in the underworld was not aware of the last Water and Land Assembly in the Capital Prefecture, and no ghosts would go to the city of the dead in the underworld to inform her.

“Hey, is it true? Uncle Ji, your disciple snuck away and fell in love with a mortal for decades. She thought she was hiding well, but you must have known the whole time, right? Otherwise, how could you have been so lucky as to come out when disaster was about to strike?”

“Ahem, brother, she’s his mount.”

“Hey, close enough!”

If it was the Dragon Son, it was to be expected, but he hadn’t expected the Dragon Daughter to also be fond of gossip today. Ji Yuan simply ignored them. After all, there was no way he could go into detail about this matter.

The Xiao family residence was actually on Yongning Street, but Ji Yuan had divined that the young master of the Xiao family was not in the mansion at present, but instead was in a restaurant on Xining Avenue with the real Hongxiu.

When Ji Yuan and the Dragon Children approached the restaurant, they actually spotted an acquaintance.

The Head of the Astronomical Department, Yan Chang, was supporting a drunk man with a short beard. The two walked out of the restaurant together. The second man was still shouting that he wanted to continue drinking.

Yan Chang was thin but strong. He lifted the man’s arm onto his shoulders and led him out.

It was already dark, but not many people were coming and going near the restaurant.

“Brother Yan… Brother Yan, let’s go to my house, let’s drink, let’s keep drinking…”

“Okay, okay, Lord1 Yu is in such a good mood, I will definitely accompany you to the end — hey, watch your feet.”

The drunk man laughed.

“Hahahaha… It must be fate to meet Lord Yan here. Today I will take out my treasured Aged Golden Jade Wine…”

“Master Yu, you are too polite!”

Yan Chang supported the man as they walked, and inadvertently looked up and saw Ji Yuan not too far away. He was was obviously stunned for a moment.

“Mr. Ji?”

Yan Chang rubbed his eyes and looked again. It really wasn’t an illusion.

“Lord Yan, how have you been?”

“Mr. Ji, it’s really you! I… I greet Mr. Ji, and the two of you.”

Yan Chang dragged the other man into a reluctant bow. Although he looked happy, he was not overly excited.

“Ah? Why aren’t we moving? Have we reached home? Let’s go… let’s go have a drink…”

Ji Yuan nodded towards Yan Chang, and looked at the person he was supporting. He had the aura of an official, and was likely not a commoner.

“This lord seems to be very distressed?”

Yan Chang smiled helplessly.

“Mr. Ji, what do you think is the most difficult job in the world?”

“Can this be ranked?”

Ji Yuan couldn’t help but ask.

Yan Chang didn’t hold him in suspense,2 and pointed to the person next to him.

“The most difficult job in the world is being Governor of the Capital Prefecture.”

The highest officials in the government offices of the two major prefectures of Zhili Province were not called magistrates,3 as they were in other places, but rather governor,4 which was one rank higher than magistrate, and of the fourth rank, like ordinary governors5.6

The Dragon Children did not know much about human affairs, or perhaps they hadn’t yet realized it, but Ji Yuan understood as soon as he heard it, and couldn’t help but look twice at this drunkard.

The emperor was directly in charge of this government office, and if there was some special case, it might involve some high-ranking officials, or perhaps some royal relatives, were was indeed very difficult to handle.

“Lord Yan, please continue as you please. I have something else to do, so I will take my leave first.”

Ji Yuan didn’t go into the details, he just bowed and left with the two people behind him.

Yan Chang opened his mouth, but in the end he didn’t dare to call out to Ji Yuan.

By now, Emperor Yuan De’s fervor in seeking immortals and immortal medicine had greatly decreased. To be frank, Yan Chang even hoped that the old emperor would not find a true master.

Given the current situation in the Capital, if Yan Chang worked too diligently to find an immortal, Emperor Yuan De might not die before Yan Chang himself would be put in danger.

Moreover, the actions of immortals were not something that a mortal like himself could control. It was impossible for him to call them to stop.

Ji Yuan and others soon arrived at the door of the restaurant. Ying Feng was still looking at Yan Chang and the Governor of the Capital Prefecture, who were walking away.

“Uncle Ji, this Head of the Astronomical Department is quite free and casual. He didn’t come to pester you, despite the fact that he should know that you are an immortal.”

“Ah, yes, he is more sensible than His Highness Ying.”

Hearing Ji Yuan’s ridicule, the Dragon Son felt a little rare embarrassment, while the Dragon Daughter covered her mouth and snickered.

Ji Yuan didn’t say anything more and led the two of them into the restaurant one after another.

“Ah, you three guests, please come in. May I ask if you have reserved a table in this building?”

Although he was originally thinking of going to the private room on the second floor, Ji Yuan immediately changed his mind after hearing the waiter’s words.

“A reserved table…”

Ji Yuan looked up at someplace upstairs.

“It’s in your private room upstairs at the northeast corner.”

“Huh?”

The waiter froze for a moment.

“Uh, sir, are you mistaken? That private room is already occupied.”

Ji Yuan stepped into the restaurant and shook his head with a smile.

“No mistake, no mistake. It was Young Master Xiao who booked it. He arrived first, and we are the guests.”

“Oh oh oh! I understand, sir, you should have told me earlier, I’ll take you upstairs!”

“Okay, thank you.”

Ji Yuan walked up the stairs with the waiter. The Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter looked at each other, then hurriedly followed.

In the private room at the northeast corner of the second floor, Xiao Ling and the woman he loved were toasting each other and drinking together. There were two tables in the room, one with wine and food, and the other with the four treasures of the study and a newly finished painting.

While they were in high spirits, there was a knock at the door.

“Knock knock knock…”

“Master Xiao, your guests are here.”

Xiao Ling put down the cup and looked at the beautiful woman beside him in confusion.

“Who is it?”

With a “creak”, the door was pushed open, and Ji Yuan walked in. He bowed with cupped hands to Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan.

“It’s me, Mr. Xiao. Hello, Miss Duan!”

Xiao Ling didn’t know Ji Yuan at all. He was about to open his mouth to say something, but he was obviously shocked when he heard Ji Yuan’s words.

One of the hands of the woman next to him grabbed Xiao Ling’s clothes under the table, but her face was calm as she spoke before Xiao Ling could.

“Sir, I’m afraid you have made a mistake. The little girl’s surname is Lu.”

Seeing this situation, the waiter outside realized that he might have done something wrong. Just as he was about to speak, he saw the woman behind the man who had entered the room stretch out her hand and wave it across the air in front of his eyes. His mind was dizzy for a moment, and then he walked downstairs.

Ji Yuan ignored what was happening behind him and walked into the room. The Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter also came in together and closed the door. The latter even touched the door frame, and a hidden magical light flashed across the room.

Xiao Ling did not speak for a while, staring with narrowed eyes at the three strangers who had come in. He could see that these three did not know martial arts, and he could kill them with one hand.

“Who are you?”

Xiao Ling asked in a cold voice, three silver coins at the ready in his hands under the table.

“Uncle Ji, he is the one who owes you the debt? Interesting, they truly are two…”

Mortals!

Ying Feng seemed very curious. Before entering the capital, Ji Yuan told him some of the story. He was very shocked to learn that the fox demon actually tricked his uncle Ji and escaped unscathed.

“Ridiculous! When did this Lord Xiao7 owe you a debt?”

Chapter 268: The Dragon Daughter Is Angry

Hearing Xiao Ling’s angry shout, Ji Yuan answered in a serious manner.

“Just a dozen days ago, no more, no less: five hundred taels of gold.”

After he spoke, Ji Yuan took two steps closer, pulled out a stool, and sat down, then glanced under the table out of the corner of his eyes.

The Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter glanced at each other, then also walked to the side of the table and sat down. The former even sniffed the fragrance of the dishes on the table.

Xiao Ling ignored the two people next to him and just stared with narrowed eyes at Ji Yuan. As long as one wasn’t an idiot, it was clear just who was his priority. Although his actions had not been done in entire secrecy, it was still strange to be able to accurately find him at this time and place.

At this time, he finally saw the person in front of him clearly in the candlelight, especially the other party’s pair of pale eyes.

“Hmph, hah, ridiculous. Shopkeeper! Shopkeeper!”

Xiao Ling called out a few times but did not hear footsteps approaching, and immediately realized in his heart: could it be that the other party had ambushed his expert outside?

“Who are you? I can’t remember who I might have offended.”

Xiao Ling subconsciously looked at the beautiful woman beside him. The other party just called out her original surname, so perhaps he already knew who the person next to him was.

By normal logic, the other party would likely have checked the details people around him while investigating Xiao Ling’s background.

“Five hundred taels of gold is no small amount. Why don’t I remember owing this debt?”

“Of course you don’t remember, because you were not there when the debt was established.”

Ji Yuan said in a somewhat playful tone, and continued talking before Xiao Ling’s anger burst.

“It was on the large performance boat on the banks of the Shushui River in Chengsu Prefecture, You Province. I ransomed a person who looks just like Miss Duan next to you.”

As soon as these words came out, Xiao Ling suddenly stood up.

“Impossible! Over there…”

Xiao Ling stopped halfway through his words, and his expression changed several times. Duan Muwan next to him was also shocked.

Ji Yuan was in no hurry, so he waited for the other party to continue.

But Ying Feng saw that Xiao Ling didn’t continue to speak. After glancing at Ji Yuan, he couldn’t help but speak again.

“Looking at your official aura, you must be the son of an official, so you must have some means. But it is not possible for you to quietly switch the Red Beauty on the Daxiu Ship. Do you understand what I mean?”

“Haha, I do not understand!”

Xiao Ling sneered began to play dumb. His hand under the table withdrew the silver, but instead quietly took out a roll of yellow talisman paper from the hidden pocket inside his sleeve.

“By the way, sir, you said I owe you 500 taels of gold. I will pay it back now. I just happen to have a silver banknote of this full amount.”

After Xiao Ling spoke, his hands exploded with true Qi, and he suddenly flipped the Eight Immortals Table and tore open the yellow talisman in his hand.

“Answer me, Great God of Qing Prefecture!”

Swoosh-

Dazzling streams of red light burst out from Xiao Ling’s hands, as if snakes of red light were slithering away.

Xiao Ling’s next move was to hug Duan Muwan, who was beside him, and whisper “let’s go,” using his inner energy and great strength to slam sideways into the window.

“Bang!”

With this sound, Xiao Ling, who was still holding Duan Muwan, was bounced back by the wooden wall as if he had a sheet of iron. His head was dizzy.

He had expected that after he had broken through the wooden wall, he would escape with the beauty in his arms, which naturally did not happen.

“Go? Where are you going?”

Xiao Ling shook his dizzy head. His body seemed a little stiff. He turned around little by little. Even though he was mentally prepared, he couldn’t help but be shocked upon seeing situation behind him.

The Eight Immortals Table and the platters of wine and food that he had overturned were all suspended in the air. The wine had not been spilled, the dishes had not fallen, and the cups and plates were unbroken. The woman waved her arm and the wine, dishes, cups, and table returned to their original places, and everything was restored.

Ying Feng held the yellow talisman in his hand, which he had taken from Xiao Ling’s grasp.

There are a lot of messy lines drawn on it like a ghost’s painting. The only special thing were the words “Qing Prefecture” written on it. Some obscure supernatural light flowed in it. He played with it for a while then handed it to Ji Yuan, who had not touched it at all, with both hands.

“Uncle Ji, please take a look.”

Ji Yuan took it and looked it over. It was a mess of red lines, and he couldn’t make sense of it. But the words on it were somewhat similar to an Edict, despite being very different.

Although he couldn’t understand the talisman, he could still deduce a part of its effect through some of the talisman’s movements just now. The situation just now was enough to show that Xiao Ling and his companion were not innocent mortals who knew nothing.

Looking at the panicked opponent, Ji Yuan unfolded the talisman paper and asked in a low voice.

“Do you know that if three ordinary people were here, they would have been pierced through the heart by ten thousand snakes just now…”

Ji Yuan frowned, looked up at the panicked two people. Although he seemed calm, his voice was like crashing waves and thunder.

“Evil techniques are bloodthirsty, and use blood to nourish the talisman. This alone is enough to make the underworld drag your souls to their prison for re-education. Who gave you such a vicious talisman?”

Ji Yuan’s rebuke was filled with a soul-shaking momentum, making Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan feel like children who heard thunder rumbling in the night rain for the first time. Their hearts were trembled and their bodies went numb.

It took long time before Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan could react. After all, Duan Muwan didn’t have Xiao Ling’s psychological steadiness. She was terrified, and cried as she defended herself.

“This is… it’s a gift from the gods, it’s not some evil technique… We just want to be together… it’s thanks to the gods that we could be here, wuwuwuwu…”

“What gods? Which gods? The Great God of Qing Prefecture that you just called on? Speak clearly!”

Ying Feng couldn’t stand the woman’s cries.

Xiao Ling patted the frightened Duan Muwan. His face was gloomy and uncertain. He didn’t know whether the three people in the room were demons or ghosts — in short, the situation was stronger than what a mortal could deal with. He hoped the identity of the god who he had brought up could calm the other party.

“If you really want to settle accounts, go find the River Goddess of the Tongtian River!”1

“Huh?”

Ying Ruoli was puzzled.

Xiao Ling looked at her, took a breath, and continued.

“That’s right, it was the Lady Ying of the Tongtian River who helped us. Two years ago, she saw that Wan’er and I were in love but couldn’t be together. She was deeply moved and showed up to help us. She also gave us this talisman for self-defense…”

“You– What did you say!?”

Ying Ruoli couldn’t bear it anymore, and asked in a voice full of shock and anger. Her high voice startled everyone, including Ji Yuan.

She stared at the two for a while, then suddenly sneered.

“Hahaha…”

“I guess that the River Goddess must have told you that this talisman must be fed blood from time to time. Spiritual blood is the best, followed by human blood, and finally animal blood?”

“You…”

“How do I know? Hmph, by teaching people to use evil methods to feed the talisman, in the next ten years, or even decades, you will rely more and more on this talisman, because it can harm others without any price. But the appetite of the talisman will grow. If animal blood can’t feed it, it will be human blood. If there are not enough people, it will kill more…”

Ji Yuan had no idea that this talisman was so evil. He couldn’t help but break into a cold sweat hearing Ying Ruoli’s words. Her eyes had changed a little when she looked at Xiao Ling again, but now it was obviously the anger of the real River Goddess.

Both Ji Yuan and the Dragon Children could see that Xiao Ling was not lying. That’s why Ying Ruoli was even more furious.

“You said that person claimed to be the Goddess of the Tongtian River? But she gave you such an evil thing, and you believed her?”

Ying Ruoli’s imposing aura was now threatening, her divine power is flowing faintly. The two mortals were so terrified that Xiao Ling’s lips and tongues trembled as he circulated his Qi.

“This, this… At that time, the god came over the waves, and showed so many supernatural techniques. I was just a mortal. If not, how could I think to use it…”

“So you believed it? Even if she gave you such an evil talisman?”

Xiao Ling fell silent, and his eyes flickered a little. Ying Ruoli sneered repeatedly.

“It seems that you still had doubts in your heart, but you gladly accepted it. I guess she had a condition for her help, and this condition was to raise this talisman, right?”

Ying Feng looked at his little sister, who rarely grew angry, and walked over to Ji Yuan and whispered.

“Uncle Ji, Ruoli has never been so furious… She is more advanced than me in cultivation… If she loses control, we can only rely on you, Uncle Ji…”

Ji Yuan turned to look at him and then at the Dragon Daughter with a subtle expression on his face.

Ying Ruoli obviously heard what her brother said in front of Ji Yuan. She suddenly turned her head and glared at him fiercely, then smiled awkwardly at Ji Yuan.

After that, the Dragon Daughter finally eased her emotions and faced Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan again, her eyes flashing.

“That evil creature said that she was the Goddess of the Tongtian River, so guess who who I am?”

After the Dragon Daughter finished speaking, she lifted both her arms, and all the disguise changes on her body disappeared. Her face became more and more beautiful, and her clothes also showed a stream of flowing light.

In a matter of seconds, the woman who had been there just now had turned into the dignified and majestic clothes of the River Goddess, with a golden ribbon fluttering between her shoulders and sleeves like a wave.

“If you can’t guess, I can give you a hint: my name is Ying Ruoli!”

Chapter 269: The Difference Between a Righteous God and a Demon

If they had heard the name Ying Ruoli under normal circumstances, Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan would definitely not have reacted.

But just when they mentioned the Tongtian River Goddess Ying, they saw such a big reaction from this woman, and now she was showing such a supernatural change. In addition, her surname was Ying. Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan couldn’t help but naturally have a thought flash through their minds — ‘Could she be the River Goddess? ’

It was particularly strong as the woman in front of them not only had an extraordinary aura, but her appearance was also much closer to what they imagined the River Goddess to be, and far more divine.

“You… are the River Goddess?”

Xiao Ling spoke more respectfully than before. Although he was already flustered before, he was still holding onto his pride. However, faced with the powerful oppression of the Dragon Daughter, he was unable to resist her crushing majesty.

The Dragon Daughter looked at Xiao Ling indifferently.

“You have good discernment, but you knew that the talisman was evil and still kept it. You even claimed that it was the Goddess of the River who helped you, even though you doubted the other party. It seems you are an individual with evil thoughts. As a small punishment, I will take away ten years of your vitality!”

The Dragon Daughter just raised a hand and hooked a finger, and spots of white light appeared on Xiao Ling’s body, then gathered in the palm of the Dragon Daughter’s hand.

At the same time, Xiao Ling seemed to have lost all of his strength. His legs softened and he knelt down with a pale face. During this whole process, his body kept losing temperature, feeling extremely painful. This pain penetrated deep into his soul and was unbearable. He couldn’t even cry or shout.

“Minister Xiao!”

Duan Muwan, who was beside him, wanted to support Xiao Ling as he fell down, but she was too weak to hold him, so she could only accompany him in losing her balance.

“Bang…”

Xiao Ling fell to the ground, his face pale and blue, his body cold and continuously shivering.

“Minister Xiao! Minister Xiao! Are you okay? Don’t scare me!”

Duan Muwan was so panicked that she shook Xiao Ling, but got no response. She could only hug him to help keep him warm.

“Don’t scare me, Minister Xiao, say something!”

After shouting a few times, Duan Muwan seemed to have suddenly realized something. She turned around, knelt in front of the Dragon Daughter, and kowtowed repeatedly.

“Lady River Goddess! I know we were wrong, and I also know that we mistakenly believed in a demon and used things that shouldn’t be used. This matter started with me, and the mistakes we made committed together. I do not ask of you to let Minister Xiao go, I just hope that we can share the responsibility and I can share half of his pain!”

The woman’s head banged against the wooden floor of the restaurant’s second floor until her forehead was swollen. Although Xiao Ling next to her was in so much pain that he couldn’t move, he grit his teeth and tried his best to reach out and grab Duan Muwan.

The Dragon Daughter looked at the two-person scene in front of her, frowned slightly, and did not speak, but turned her head and glanced sideways at Ji Yuan and the Dragon Son behind her.

Ji Yuan looked at Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan’s state, then at the Dragon Daughter’s reaction, and essentially understood.

“Alright, River Goddess, please calm down. Miss Duan’s words are not without reason. Let’s deal with it as appropriate.”

As if she was waiting for those words, the Dragon Daughter turned around and bowed slightly to Ji Yuan, then turned back to Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan again. With a single thought, Duan Muwan was no longer able to kowtow or hit her head against the floor.

“You, Xiao Ling, are very lucky. Uncle Ji has spoken for you, and you two have some sincere feelings…”

Ying Ruoli looked at Duan Muwan carefully.

“In that case, I will fulfill your wish!”

Xiao Ling was cold, weak, and turning purple. He could only keep breathing heavily as he tried to catch Duan Muwan.

The Dragon Daughter glanced at him and spread out her right hand again. Points of white light flew back into Xiao Ling’s body, which had collapsed to the ground. At the same time, some points of white light also emerged from Duan Muwan’s body and flew into the Dragon Daughter’s hands.

After about two breaths, all the light disappeared, and the Dragon Daughter closed her hand and tucked it into her gorgeous wide sleeves.

Xiao Ling felt that he had regained his strength. Although his body was a little weak, he was no longer so weak that he could not stand. Seeing that Duan Muwan was swaying beside him, he immediately stood up and supported her.

“Wan’er, why are you so stupid! Why should such a weak woman like you bear such pain…”

The Dragon Daughter looked at them, nodded in the direction of Ji Yuan and the Dragon Son, and took a step back, indicating that she would no longer care about this issue anymore.

Ji Yuan looked at Ying Ruoli with an inexplicable expression. She did not hold back the first time, but the second time, she did not really let Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan split the burden, but returned 90% of the vitality and only symbolically took another 10% from Duan Muwan.

Otherwise, with Duan Muwan’s physical fitness, she would have been unable to hold on and would have collapsed. Five years of vitality1 could not be simply converted to five years of predestined lifespan.

There was no Book of Life and Death2 in this world. Even the underworld could only see the changes in their records when a person was about to die. It was impossible to change a person’s lifespan just by drawing it out. Ten or five years of vitality drew out the vital energy of the human body, which was a fundamental aspect. Years were just the measurement method. If an accident happened during recuperation, it would be easy for evil Qi to enter the body and breed serious illness.

Ji Yuan stood up from the stool in front of the table, walked around the table, and came to the two who were sitting on the ground hugging each other, which caused Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan to look up at him.

“Since Young Master Xiao has the wisdom to judge people’s character, it’s best not to choose to ignore some things for the sake of profit. A scholar should have some backbone. You were much better seven or eight years ago than you were two years ago.”

“Seven or eight years ago?”

Xiao Ling looked up at this inscrutable gentleman in green robes, and the doubts in his heart were revealed in his voice.

Ji Yuan nodded.

“Back then, on the Tongtian River in winter, at the stern of the Xiao family’s tower boat, Young Master Xiao and your father had a big quarrel, and it was also because of Miss Hongxiu. In my impression, Young Master Xiao was very ambitious.”

“You…”

“Don’t you remember me?”

Ji smiled.

“Young Master Xiao, you and your father did comment on me that day, but it’s normal for you not to remember a mere commoner who works hard making a living, rowing by on the river.”

Xiao Ling’s eyes widened, and he blurted his words.

“You were the fisherman who rowed the boat back then!?”

“Indeed.”

Ji Yuan nodded and reached out to help Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan from the ground.

Xiao Ling’s expression was a little dazed. He suddenly recalled the moment all the way at the beginning, when he decided to pass the exam and score highly, when he wanted to get a position that even his father had to look up to, there was a moment when the fisherman in the boat turned around and looked back, nodding at him from a distance.

This moment had long been forgotten, but at this moment it was immediately brought back to his mind.

“Seeing that you two are sincere and affectionate, Goddess Jiang has already shown mercy. This is the difference between a righteous god and a demon. One will stop, while the other will give you a talisman that will eventually harm others and yourself. Young Master Xiao, you should act more correctly in the future.”

Hearing Ji Yuan’s words, Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan subconsciously looked at Ying Ruoli. The latter just glanced at them and closed her eyes, tacitly acquiescing to Ji Yuan’s words.

Xiao Ling took a deep breath, bowed with cupped hands to the Dragon Daughter, then gave Ji Yuan a bow as well.

Ji Yuan nodded and held Xiao Ling’s hand, preventing him from bowing too deeply.

“The one who deceived you is a great demon from another continent. Her actions are mysterious and unpredictable. I think she has big plans. I too have been fooled by her. You two are just ordinary people, sometimes you don’t have much choice even if you can see through her. This time, I will treat it as you being helpless.”

Although Ji Yuan said these words, this matter had been handled by him and the Dragon Daughter alone. The underworld of the Capital Prefecture, who was originally unaware of these events, would definitely leave a mark on his record afterwards. If he didn’t accumulate any good deeds in the future, he would still be destroyed after death, but Ji Yuan did not reveal this.

Xiao Ling smiled bitterly, and bowed once again to the gentleman in the green robe in front of him.

“Thank you, Mr. Ji, for your advice!”

At this point, Xiao Ling was certain that the person who had come now was a true immortal. His actions and methods were completely different from the “River Goddess” he had met before. Even his intuition agreed that these people were all virtuous and even gave off a sense of purity and warmth.

“Mr. Ji, I wonder which country she is from? If that monster can make you suffer… will it harm the people of Dazhen? I have held this talisman for two years, what impact will it have?”

Xiao Ling felt uneasy, but he still asked.

“Hah…hahahahaha…”

The Dragon Son couldn’t help but laugh when he heard Xiao Ling’s words. The Dragon Daughter’s expression was a little bit tense, but she only barely maintained her sternness.

Ying Feng nodded to Xiao Ling.

“You… hahaha… Make Uncle Ji suffer? The world is so big, there must certainly be some people who have this ability, but they definitely don’t include that white fox. She was just lucky and escaped from Uncle Ji’s grasp. It was also because Uncle Ji has a good temper and likes to take his time with everything. If it were my father…”

“Ahem-hem!”

The Dragon Daughter couldn’t help but let out two coughs, and the Dragon Son hurriedly shut up.

This place was not too far from the Tongtian River, and his father was sleeping. Perhaps he would dream about some fragments of these events after they invoked his name, which would be bad.

Ji Yuan looked back at the Dragon Son. This guy was far more relaxed around him than around the Old Dragon. He thought about what had happened today: should he “accidentally” let these events slip when he found an opportunity to chat with the Old Dragon someday?

Ji Yuan looked at Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan again. Sure enough, the look in his eyes became even awed.

“Young Master Xiao, don’t worry, you are all right now.”

“Sir, since you have such great powers, the other part should be able to surrender even if they are in another country. The people of Dazhen are innocent…”

Seeing that Xiao Ling could still say these words at this time, although he seemed to be making unfounded claims, he still dared to say it, after all, so Ji Yuan raised his estimation of him.

Although the pronunciation of the characters was the same, Ji Yuan understood that Xiao Ling said “country” instead of “continent.”3 He shook his head and explained with a smile.

“Young Master Xiao, the continent I refer to is not one of the thirteen provinces of Dazhen, nor is it a prefecture of Dazhen. This world is much bigger than you know!”

元阳 (yuányáng): Taoist metaphysical concept of vital Yang energy, a more fundamental life force than just time.

A book in Chinese mythology that records the birth, lifespan, and destined death of all mortal creatures in the world. Most prominently referenced in Journey to the West, where Sun Wukong erases his name and the names of every monkey he knows from the book, making them immortal

州 (zhōu): state/country, 洲 (zhōu): continent. Pronounced the same, same tones, they even look similar!

Chapter 270: The Emperor Will Soon Die, What Did He Say?

Not in Dazhen?

Xiao Ling was stunned. In the surrounding countries and even in the distant country of Tianbao, most places had similar names for prefectures and capitals, and only a few barbarians were called tribes. If it were merely a foreign country, the Immortal would not have explained it like this.

He wanted to ask again, but Ji Yuan had already begun speaking to preempt him.

“Young Master Xiao, don’t worry about those things outside of your influence. Don’t forget that I am here to collect on a debt today.”

Although only a moment had passed, many things had happened in that short time. If Ji Yuan hadn’t said anything, Xiao Ling would have completely forgotten about it, or thought that the debt they mentioned earlier was due to what the River Goddess had done.

At this moment, when he heard the immortal in front of him talking about collecting debts, Xiao Ling thought for a while, then asked to confirm.

“Five hundred taels of gold?”

“That’s right, five hundred taels of gold, Young Master Xiao will be able to procure it, right?”

Based on Ji Yuan’s understanding of the Xiao family, Xiao Ling’s father was not so honest of a person; even if he were an honest official, he could still come up with five hundred taels of gold after so many years of salary.

“Okay, sir. Will you be coming home with me, or do you have other plans?”

Ji Yuan nodded.

“No need, Young Master Xiao, you can go back to your mansion on your own, take the gold, and send it back. We will wait for the Young Master here.”

Xiao Ling looked at Duan Muwan.

“What about Wan’er?”

Ji Yuan understood what he meant by this question, but he did not intend to restrict their personal freedom.

“You two can do whatever you want.”

Hearing this, Xiao Ling felt more at ease. After saying goodbye to Ji Yuan and the Dragon Children, he led Duan Muwan out of the restaurant and left into the night.

Ji Yuan had already sat back at the table, while the Dragon Son looked through the window at the two people walking away, before sitting down.

“Hahaha… Since he was fortunate enough to meet Uncle Ji at the very beginning, and still left such an impression, if he relied on his own hard work, meeting him again so many years later might have been his good fortune. It’s really ironic… “

Ying Ruoli also nodded in agreement. She and her brother had already understood much of Uncle Ji’s temper.

As the two siblings sighed, Ji Yuan had already taken three new pairs of chopsticks and handed them to the Dragon Son and Dragon Daughter respectively.

“There is still a table of wine and food, don’t waste it.”

There were seven or eight dishes on the table, all of which were rich and hearty. Xiao Ling and Duan Muwan would definitely not eat them tonight, so Ji Yuan did not stand on ceremony.

As soon as Ji Yuan started eating, the Dragon Children had to accompany their elder, even if they were just pretending, so they naturally picked up their chopsticks and started eating.

Half an hour passed before Xiao Ling came back. He returned to the private room carrying a small box in his arms, but Duan Muwan did not come with him.

Originally, he could have used silver banknotes, but Xiao Ling realized that what the other party wanted was gold, so he decided to just bring real gold instead.

“Mr. Ji, five hundred taels of gold, exactly!”

Xiao Ling put the small wooden box on the corner of the table and opened it to reveal the neatly arranged gold ingots inside.

The size of the box was about the size of a child’s shoebox from Ji Yuan’s previous life. If the five hundred taels of gold inside were melted together, they wouldn’t even be as big as a brick.

But even such a small box was heavy with the five hundred taels. Xiao Ling’s body was relatively weak at the moment. Even though he had good martial arts, he was still sweating and panting after carrying the box for so long.

Ji Yuan just glanced at the golden objects in the box, and then nodded.

“Okay, that seems correct. Young Master Xiao and I are even now.”

After he spoke, Ji Yuan waved his arm, and the box on the table moved without a hint of wind. The gold spun in the air, flowed into his sleeves, and disappeared without a trace.

After recovering the debt and figuring out what the white fox did, Ji Yuan didn’t want to argue with Xiao Ling any more. After all of these actions and exchanging a final bow, he took the Dragon Children, bid farewell, and took his leave.

Xiao Ling looked through the window in the private room to see where the people were going, but after only a dozen steps, the three of them had disappeared into the night.

“Uncle Ji, the Xiao family is descended from Xiao Jing, right?”

The Dragon Daughter suddenly asked as they walked along the road. Ji Yuan looked at her and nodded.

“That’s right. I wasn’t sure before I came here, but after seeing Xiao Ling and observing his aura, I can confirm that he is a descendant of Xiao Jing.”

Ji Yuan knew that the Dragon Daughter had read Wang Li’s story on the boat before, and the summary at the end gave a general overview of the whole tale, so it was not surprising that she knew about Xiao Jing.1

This made the Dragon Son curious.

“Ruoli, what are you and Uncle Ji talking about? Who is Xiao Jing? Why do you know about it but I don’t? I’ve been with Uncle Ji much longer than you!”

Ji Yuan was too lazy to explain to him. He gave the Dragon Daughter a wink and walked ahead alone, letting the brother and sister talk amongst themselves.

Time passed, crossing through summer and entering the autumn season again. Ji Yuan has been alone in the Capital Prefecture for more than two months.

During this period of time, Ji Yuan also learned a few things. The situation in the capital was naturally tense, but whether it was intrigue or scheming, none had any effect on him.

In his spare time, he went to the various chess halls in the capital to watch other people play chess. He also went to the city of the dead in the underworld to visit Bai Ruo and her husband.

Another thing Ji Yuan found worth noting was Yin Qing’s imperial examination ranking.

The results of this year’s spring examination had been announced as early as when the apricot blossoms were in full bloom in spring. Yin Qing, who had already passed the provincial examination and obtained the qualification for the palace examination, also participated in the palace examination.

His results were not that high, and he could not even reach the top three rankings, but in fact it wasn’t too bad. He was just behind them, in 4th place.2 It was just because his father Yin Zhaoxian was too dazzling that his son Yin Qing’s results were said to be poor.

This was actually quite interesting. Others might not recognize it, but Ji Yuan understood Yin Qing’s talent.

It could be said that the positions of Jieyuan, Huiyuan, and Zhuangyuan3 required some luck to obtain, and it was normal not to get them, but Yin Qing was still capable of competing for the top three spots in the palace examination, and his chances of attaining it were quite high.

However, Yin Qing’s results were just at the bottom of the 2nd rank, barely prevented from falling into 3rd rank, which was very interesting.

It was naturally impossible to operate behind the scenes. Even though Yin Zhaoxian had made many enemies, no one dared to provoke him. Ji Yuan could only conclude that his “Xiao Yin Qing” was deliberately hiding with his shortcomings.

Ji Yuan could even guess what Yin Qing was thinking: basically as long as he could meet the requirements to become an official, and then use some connections to get a suitable position, he could play a key role without standing out too much for the time being.

Just as how many people respected Yin Zhaoxian, many people were also afraid of him. But in truth, although Yin Zhaoxian was a talented and capable minister, Yin Qing, who was more of a scholar and had a delicate mind, was the one they should pay more attention to. But who else could know this aside from those who were close to him?

On this day, Yin Zhaoxian, who had worked hard for many years in Wan Province, was summoned to the capital to report on his duties.

All the officials in the court and in the public, including Yin Zhaoxian, knew that time was running out for the current emperor.

After all, regardless of whether they respected Yin Zhaoxian, feared him, or even resented him, the civil and military officials, nobles, and ministers of the court knew very well that Yin Zhaoxian was a great minister, talented, wise, and even loyal.

In recent years, everyone in the court had been in a dangerous position. There were perhaps only one and a half officials who Emperor Yuande truly trusted, and were extremely favored. One was Yin Zhaoxian. The half was the Head of the Astronomical Observations Department Yan Chang, who had truly found an immortal fate for the emperor several times.

Yin Zhaoxian, the governor of Wan Province, hurrying to the capital at this time had a more symbolic meaning.

At this moment, outside the emperor’s inner chamber, deep in the palace, an old eunuch walked into the bedroom with small steps. He came to the emperor’s bedside, bowed, and whispered into the curtain.

“Your Majesty, the governor of Wan Province, Yin Zhaoxian, has entered the capital, and is waiting outside the palace.”

“Good… Good Minister Yin is here? How long has it been?”

The old eunuch had served the the emperor for a long time and knew what he was asking.

“Reporting to His Majesty, it has only been a little over a month since the imperial edict was issued. Yunbo Prefecture in Wan Province is a long way from the capital. Lord Yin is worthy of being a pillar of loyalty and patriotism. He has been traveling day and night for more than ten days, and ran several good horses to death…”

“Ho… alright, good… Call him in.”

“Yes!”

Loud noises were forbidden in the bedroom these days. Not long after the old eunuch left, he returned and led a dusty Yin Zhaoxian to the bedside.

“Yin Zhaoxian, governor of Wan Province, pays respect to His Majesty!”

Yin Zhaoxian bowed deeply.

“My Dear Minister Yin… come closer… let… let me see you…”

Yin Zhaoxian glanced at the old eunuch beside him, then without hesitation, took five steps forward and knelt down on one knee, bringing his face slightly lower than the bed. The old eunuch walked to the imperial bed and opened the curtains.

At this moment, the old emperor’s face appeared thin and pale, and Yin Zhaoxian was slightly taken aback. He had not been to the capital in many years, and his impression of the old emperor was still from when he had won first place in the imperial examination. He did not expect that he would now be a completely different person.

But his daze lasted only a moment. Yin Zhaoxian hurriedly bowed his head and saluted.

“Your Majesty!”

In the eyes of the old emperor, seeing Yin Zhaoxian was completely different from seeing other ministers. It seemed as though Yin Zhaoxian’s whole body was brighter, especially compared to other places in the palace, which showed an illusion of darkness.

“Dear Minister Yin, I have heard rumors from the markets… from the scholars… many… many people have heard that you, Good Minister, are of noble and virtuous character… and you… you are the leader of the age…”

“I dare not bear such an evaluation!”

The old emperor smiled, and with the help of several eunuchs, he sat up from the bed with pillows and other objects placed behind him.

“Please take a seat.”

“Yes!”

The eunuch brought over a small low chair, and Yin Zhaoxian sat down calmly after thanking him.

“Hooh… hooh… Originally, I thought it was just a rumor… but after seeing you today, Good Minister… I am a little bit convinced!”

“I fear not!”

“Hahaha… Others might be truly fearful, but you are not!”

This time Yin Zhaoxian was really panicked and hurriedly bowed.

Yin Zhaoxian bowed slightly.

“Whatever Your Majesty asks, this minister shall tell you everything I know!”

The old eunuch who was bringing over some tea trembled and nearly dropped the tray.

Chapter 271: People’s Hearts Are Unpredictable

When this question came up, not only did the old eunuch’s hands tremble, but so did Yin Zhaoxian’s body and mind. Could such a question be answered casually?

Yin Zhaoxian got down from the chair almost the very instant after he heard those words, and knelt directly in front of the bed, cupping his hands up by his head and bowing with his head lowered all the way down.

Yin Zhaoxian spoke these words quickly and clearly, and did not dare to look up to see Emperor Yuan De’s expression.
