YF: “wait how do you know how the emperor died?”

JY: “oh dw I watched it happen”

YF: O_O

But like… for EVERY SINGLE major event that’s happened in the entire span of time since you’ve seen him. I’m cracking up here, this is too funny

The tassel of the sword is a decoration meant to enhance the visuals of the movements, not for any practical purpose. JY is saying that YF has grown out of the emphasis on visual beauty and aesthetics in favor of practicality.

荣 (róng): glory/honor, 楼 (lóu): root/source –> “Source of Glory,” frankly an AWESOME D&D-esque name for a restaurant. Technically speaking the word “Restaurant” is still the same word that I previously translated as “Building,” but it works differently here in context

I have been translating the title (大侠) dàxiá as “Warrior,” when in fact it can mean knight/swordsman/noble warrior/chivalrous hero. JY is saying that although they already have the title of Daxia, perhaps they can truly be worthy of the “noble hero” part of the meaning, instead of just being “Swordsman”

T/n: rabbit hole time! The consumption of horse meat varies quite a bit depending on what region of the world you’re in. In most English-speaking countries, it’s generally considered a taboo. It is quite a common delicacy in Central Asia, South America, France, and especially in certain parts of Italy.

Reminder that Yuan De is a courtesy title, like how Prince Jin is a title as well

It’s an ironic euphemism for “crude or base practices”

Chapter 298: The Encounter at Shigang

Ji Yuan could soar far away with just a light touch down on some rooftops, and could travel extremely quickly.

Aside from that initial vertical leap, Yan Fei ran wildly along the rooftops. Although his steps were light, his speed was still not as fast as Ji Yuan’s, so he couldn’t catch up.

Fortunately, no matter how far away Yuanqi Inn was located, it was still located in the relatively small Nandao County. Before long, Ji Yuan had already reached the neighboring rooftop by the inn, and Yan Fei barely managed to catch up with him.

There was also a clear difference in the way the two landed. Ji Yuan landed in the tiles without making a single sound, but although Yan Fei’s Qinggong was quite skilled, he still made a little noise.

“Mr. Ji, what happened…”

Ji Yuan didn’t turn around, and raised his hand to stop Yan Fei from speaking. With his supernatural eyes, he could see a faint evil aura lingering in the inn opposite, and his nose could also pick up the faint scent of blood.

“There is indeed a demonic aura ahead. Warrior Yan, your martial arts may be exceptional, but you are no match for a demonic being. Don’t be impatient.”

“Demonic aura?”

Yan Fei was a little horrified at Ji Yuan’s serious words. He naturally thought back to the ferocious tiger spirit he had encountered that year.

He asked himself if he would have had any chance of winning against that tiger spirit even with his current martial arts, but he could only conclude that he would have been merely less embarrassed than he had been back then.

Hearing Yan Fei’s startled words, Ji Yuan nodded slightly.

“The demonic being has left, let’s go into the inn to have a look.”

With that, Ji Yuan jumped off the roof and headed towards the Yuanqi Inn. Yan Fei regained his senses and quickly followed.

There was no one manning the counter of the inn as Ji Yuan and Yan Fei walked inside. However, the people around seemed to be accustomed to women’s screams in Yuanqi Inn at this time, so no one noticed anything unusual in such a short period of time. Furthermore, there might not even had been guests — this place looked deserted.

“Mr. Ji, be careful, it shouldn’t be so quiet here!”

Yan Fei had already adjusted his grip on his sword. There was something wrong with the atmosphere here.

Ji Yuan gave a faint hum and led the way upstairs. Yan Fei was taken aback again and hurriedly followed. Before going upstairs, he glanced at the counter, only to find the innkeeper collapsed on the ground behind it.

The Yuanqi Inn’s layout had a central corridor down the middle, and rooms on both sides. Reaching the second floor, Ji Yuan quickly and purposefully strode to the right right side door at the end of the corridor.

He reached out and pushed lightly on the door of the room. It wasn’t latched.

His nose twitched slightly, and his eyes swept around. Ji Yuan’s gaze fixed on a strand of dark brown hair lying on the ground. He picked it up and saw that it was about the length of his forearm.

‘How could an ordinary monster leave hair behind? ‘

Ji Yuan frowned in thought. Not even Hu Yun would shed his hair casually. Lu Shanjun, for example, only shed hair after reaching a certain stage of cultivation. The hair in his hand looked particularly strange: it wasn’t just a single strand, but a clump.

At this moment, Yan Fei was gripping the hilt of his sword in one hand and the scabbard in the other, ready to draw his blade at any time. He quietly pushed open the doors to all the rooms in the corridor with the tip of his scabbard, then came to Ji Yuan’s side a moment later.

“Mr. Ji, all the people in the rooms have passed out. The third floor hasn’t been checked yet.”

“No need to check. It’ll be the same.”

Ji Yuan looked at the thing in his palm and casually replied. Yan Fei’s attention was drawn to the object in Ji Yuan’s hand.

“Is there something wrong with this hair?”

“To be precise, it’s a whole lock of hair, and it is no ordinary human hair.”

Ji Yuan glanced at Yan Fei and calculated a divination with his fingers as he spoke.

“I’ll chase after them and see. Warrior Yan, you can report this to the police first, or take care of the unconscious people in the inn.”

“Huh? Chase? How will you chase them?”

But before Yan Fei could get an answer, Ji Yuan had already opened the window in the room, and jumped out with a powerful leap.

When Yan Fei rushed to the window and looked out, but even after searching for a while, he couldn’t see Ji Yuan on any of the nearby streets or rooftops. He looked up and saw a small figure rapidly disappearing in the northeastern direction, just as he was about to vanish from sight.

‘No way…so fast? ‘

He glanced at the situation in the inn behind him.

“Call the police? What good are the authorities of this damn place!”

Before he had even finished speaking, Yan Fei had already jumped out of the window, pushed off the roof opposite more than ten steps, then leapt into the air, chasing after Ji Yuan to the northeast.

Unfortunately, no matter how fluid his movements, in moments Ji Yuan’s figure had completely disappeared from his sight. He could only aim himself in the right direction and chase after him.

Northeast of Nandao County, after Ji Yuan jumped over of the city wall, he used the Soaring Dragon flight technique to move quickly.

After leaving the city, the demonic aura was still relatively weak, but he could feel that it had just passed through here not long ago. He did not blindly chase after him, but rather suddenly accelerated when he had reached a suitable distance.

Using a simple method to control the wind, combined with the Soaring Dragon technique, it seemed as though he were carried on a gust of wind, and moved quickly.

Whooosh… whoosh…

The slight whistling sound of the wind was accompanied by the faint rustling of withered and fallen leaves, but because Ji Yuan himself had an immaculate body, not much dust was raised, as if the breeze had turned his body into a lightless shadow. Soon, he moved even further ahead.

A gentle breeze swept past a scattered rocky hillock in the northeast of Nandao County. Ji Yuan had already stopped here, his gaze fixed on the southwest. Finally, he could discern a “smoke-like” demonic aura approaching, but could see no silhouette.

After slightly widening his supernatural eyes, he finally made out a blurry figure, like rippling water, at the source of the “smoke.” It looked like one person running while carrying another over their shoulder.

‘An illusion? ‘

Being able to maintain a human form, using deceptive tricks to move quickly, and exuding a little demonic aura… this must be a highly skilled demon. He had assumed it was just a minor evil creature, but now it seemed that he would be a formidable opponent if Ji Yuan did not use his ultimate weapons.

After some thought, Ji Yuan feigned composure and sat down on a rock, eyes slightly closed. Though seemingly resting, he was actually paying keen attention to the situation and any movement.

In such desolate wilderness, a refined gentleman in a white robe sitting alone on a rocky hilltop seemed unusual, but that would only be the case for an ordinary person. The approaching demonic being paid no attention to this figure who appeared mortal — not to mention that the other party couldn’t possibly see him.

As the blurry shadow drew closer, reaching the rocky hill, Ji Yuan opened his eyes and looked at the demon to his left, his gaze unerringly following the opponent’s movements.

Naturally, the demonic being had noticed this strange behavior. As he carried the person on his back, he inevitably glanced at the man. The moment the man’s gaze met his own, the demonic being suddenly realized that the other party could see him.

At such a close distance, with Ji Yuan’s supernatural eyes fully open, he could clearly discern the condensed demonic aura of the other being: it was a bull with long, curved horns.

“Bull spirits1 are quite rare.”

Just this indifferent sentence made the monster, who was about to charge over the stone hill, stop abruptly. The other party had not only seen through his concealment spell, but also his true form?

He obviously wanted to continue running, but his pause was a subconscious reaction. Once he stopped, he wanted to figure out some answers.

Ji Yuan’s face was impassive, his body motionless, his head tilted to the side. He looked at the demonic being, who seemed neither majestic nor burly, but rather more like a farmer.

“Who are you, Sir? Were you waiting for me here on purpose?”

The monster asked Ji Yuan solemnly.

Ji Yuan didn’t speak right away. He looked him up and down, before answering a different question.

“Your little illusion trick is alright, but there are very few ghosts and gods here. What are you hiding from? Just ordinary people? And who is that woman you are carrying?”

‘Aren’t there people like you here?’

The Bull Spirit naturally kept silent about his thoughts, instead speaking coldly.

“You look like a cultivator. What, are you trying to subdue demons?”

Even as he spoke, the bull demon was secretly on guard. This man had appeared on this rocky hilltop out of nowhere. Even now, he couldn’t detect any traces of the supernatural on him. He looked like a mere mortal, but that was clearly impossible.

Ji Yuan still didn’t answer the bull demon’s question, and instead replied with something irrelevant. But this time, there was was an extra strand of hair lying in his hand.

“This is bull hair, is it not? Why was it in Yuanqi Inn? I am very curious, I am merely asking for an explanation!”

When Ji Yuan took out the strand of hair, the bull demon’s pupils shrank slightly, and even subconsciously glanced around.

“You! You just came from the inn in the city?”

It could also be translated as “demon,” but given that I previously translated Lu Shan’s title as Tiger Spirit I figured this would fit better. I will be using “Spirit” and “Demon” interchangeably for the bull guy going forward, Spirit being an official title and demon being the classification, depending on context.

Chapter 299: Let’s See Whether You Survive

Ji Yuan was a little surprised by this bull demon’s reaction.

Even if he had been to the inn, and although the bull demon was by no means slow, he also hadn’t used any sophisticated escape techniques, so there were plenty of ways to catch up with him. However, it was possible that he believed Ji Yuan was waiting here specifically for him, and had some mistunderstandings.

“I have been to Yuanqi Inn in the city…”

Hearing Ji Yuan’s admission, the bull demon breathed a sigh of relief but still asked.

“Then where did you get this hair? Was it in the inn?”

At this, Ji Yuan was a little puzzled.

“You didn’t realize that some of your hair fell in the inn?”

Unexpectedly, after Ji Yuan finished his question, the bull demon immediately responded.

“Oh, that’s good, you got it from the inn, I thought… well, you really don’t know whose hair it is?”

The two looked at each other in silence for a while.

Seeing Ji Yuan’s calm face, with no ripples in his eyes, the bull demon was inexplicably relieved.

“Phew… A human cultivator with a peaceful composure like yourself would not lie so casually. It seems that you really didn’t meet her, let alone know her!”

Ji Yuan was somewhat confused. Although his heart was full of doubts, he only showed a frown on his face.

“Why do you say that? Who are you afraid of?”

At that, Ji Yuan suddenly had a realization. This bull demon did not ride on a demonic wind or perform any kind of swift escape. Instead, he used a techniques that were slightly beyond ordinary concealment techniques to hide and sprint along the ground. Ji Yuan looked at the hair in his hand and blurted out.

“You have traveled like this all this time. Are you hiding from her?”

“If I weren’t avoiding her, then who would I have been avoiding? You?”

The bull demon said sarcastically, looking Ji Yuan up and down.

“A piece of advice, sir, hurry up and throw away this unlucky hair immediately. That creature is not to be trifled with. That hair is like a living thing; the longer it grows, the closer she is. I’m leaving!”

The bull demon pointed to the back of his neck, and prepared to start moving again. As he passed by, Ji Yuan took a closer look at the back of his neck and saw a tuft of fine dark brown hair growing there. Its color was similar to the one in Ji Yuan’s hand.

It looked as though the dark brown hair in Ji Yuan’s hands had been ripped off directly from the bull demon’s neck.

“You don’t seem to be a violent and evil individual, but why are you carrying a woman on your shoulders? If you want to flee an enemy, wouldn’t carrying someone be burdensome?”

Ji Yuan stood up, and with just one step, he was swept away by the breeze and arrived in front of the bull demon, blocking his path.

The bull demon dodged and continued forward, but was blocked by Ji Yuan again. He tried to change direction several times to no avail.

“Are you done yet? Do you think you’re so great? As an immortal cultivator, can you even understand the insidious traps of mortals? They attacked me first. Since they had bad intentions, they deserve death. I will neither eat her nor kill her. I’ll let her go when the time comes.”

“Then why did you capture her?”

Ji Yuan asked in surprise.

“I… I’m taking her to teach her a lesson! I was the victim, caught in a scheme, so why I can’t teach her a lesson? It’s unreasonable! Don’t keep trying to stop me, or I’ll take action.”

Ji Yuan’s gaze grew increasingly suspicious. He couldn’t help but inspect the bull demon again. His gaze paused for a moment on the bull demon’s face, before it was drawn to the tuft of hair at the back of its neck. He noticed, strangely enough, that it was growing longer.

The hair was growing in a strange way: not only was it rapid, but it was also twisting and emerging from the collar of his robe on its own, creating a terrifying sight.

“No, I can’t hold back anymore, she must be here!”

The bull demon screamed in alarm. He stretched out a hand and ripped out the hair from the back of his neck, discarding it carelessly by the roadside. He breathed out a cloud of green gas into his palm and clasped it to the back of his neck. A faint flash of light suppressed the hair’s rapid growth.

Ji Yuan was stunned for a moment, then suddenly sensed something. Bewildered, he turned around and looked back in the direction he came from.

“Oh no! Yan Fei!”

Ji Yuan didn’t bother to waste any more words on the bull demon. The latter seemed to be preoccupied. In an instant, Ji Yuan had approached him.

The Green Vine Sword’s energy emerged from behind him. With a “buzz,” the sharp sound echoed, and with Ji Yuan’s determination to guide it, the bull demon’s supernatural concealment technique was instantly shattered. Taking advantage of having caught the bull demon off guard, Ji Yuan flicked his sleeve and snatched the woman from his shoulder.

Without stopping, Ji Yuan turned around, rushed past the bull demon, and sped back the way he had come. With the Soaring dragon technique, swept away by the gentle breeze, his speed was unbelievably fast.

“Bastard! You, a cultivator, dare to rob women!”1

The bull demon behind him was furious. Bursts of hot steam came out from his nostrils. Ji Yuan just glanced back at him as he departed, to lazy to bother with his nonsense. He didn’t even pause.

This bull demon was not a bad individual by nature, and he did not harbor much hostility in his body. Moreover, his cultivation was not shallow. If Ji Yuan wanted to fight him, it would take a lot of effort. Crucially, if he didn’t use his trump cards, he likely would not be able to subdue him. And if he used his trump card, Ji Yuan would most likely kill him. Now that Yan Fei might be in trouble, Ji Yuan would naturally rush back first.

“You… You… You…”

Seeing that Ji Yuan ignored him completely and had run far away in an instant, the bull demon hurried to catch up, but hesitated and touched the back of his neck. After this hesitation, by the time he reacted, Ji Yuan was almost gone.

Some ten li northeast of Nandao County, Yan Fei was engaged din battle.

There were currently four strange men surrounding him, each with long nails and shriveled faces. Although they were sluggish, they were not slow in their movements, jumping up and down and swiping madly to attack Yan Fei.

Yan Fei spun around, and swung the longsword in his hand left and right, exuding a fierce murderous aura. He completed six or seven strikes in an instant.

“Clang-clang-clang…”

“Pfft-pfft…”

After parrying two of the beings’ claw attacks, Yan Fei opened several deep gashes on one of their bodies, but the other party didn’t seem to be afraid of pain at all. Even with such injuries, it still attacked Yan Fai as usual, even directly going to grapple him.

Yan Fei slid back four to five feet on the balls of his feet, swinging his sword in the process and scattering flashes of sword strikes.

“Clang-clang-clang…”

“Are you human or ghosts? Why are you attacking me?”

Yan Fei asked, but before he had time to catch his breath, his opponent attacked once more. He had no time to recover, and could only raise his sword to fight again.

These monsters were incredibly fast, and oblivious to pain. Yan Fei was flustered at first, but had now adapted to their attack. Seeing their disorganized movements and sluggish reactions, he grew more adept at handling them.

After a powerful leap and somersault, Yan Fei turned around, swung his sword in mid-air, and decapitated two of the monsters.

The two headless bodies swayed briefly before collapsing to the ground.

‘Good, they’re dead!’

Seeing the corpses lying on the ground, Yan Fei was relieved. Two were already dead, and the remaining two would be much easier to deal with.

Just as he was thinking this, he suddenly smelled a strange fragrance wafting to his nose, and his vision immediately blurred. However, Yan Fei’s experience gained from years of fighting in the martial arts world had formed a conditioned reflex.

At this moment, his movements exploded, and he leapt to the side with dexterity skills. However, while he was still in the air, he felt a rope wrap around his waist, and his body was yanked in another direction.

In the moment his body changed directions, Yan Fei’s hand had already begun to swing the sword in front of him even before his sight returned. However, the feel of the blade was like cutting extremely tough leather, and he couldn’t slice through at all.

Only then did Yan Fei look down to see what was wrapped around his waist.

‘Hair!? ‘

Yan Fei was still confused when a pleasant female voice sounded.

“Before catching that old cow, I managed to catch a prickly pretty boy like you, hahahahaha…”

A little further away, a woman was unexpectedly sitting in a large locust tree at the edge of the woods. Her hair was incredibly long, and a strand had reached out and wrapped around Yan Fei, rapidly dragging him towards her.

As Yan Fei looked at her, the woman glanced back. Their eyes met and Yan Fei felt a wave of confusion return, the peculiar fragrance in his nose growing stronger.

A ghost? A demon?

Various thoughts flashed through Yan Fei’s mind. With his glimmer of clarity, he tapped several acupuncture points on his body, and even channeled Qi to seal his nostrils.

“It’s no use, it’s no use, hehehe…”

Yan Fei forced himself awake with his Qi, but he still felt a little groggy.

Clang—

The resounding clash of a sword echoed, and Yan Fei’s eyes were filled with silver light.

“Aaaaahh…”

Yan Fei suddenly heard the woman’s screams. At the same time, the tension around his body suddenly lifted, and he fell.

By the time Yan Fei landed on the ground, he had already been steadied by someone supporting him. He felt a cool, gentle breath flowing into his body from behind, instantly dispelling the drowsiness.

Yan Fei shook his head and looked behind him. Standing there was none other than Ji Yuan.

“Mr. Ji!”

“Well, you have been bewitched by an evil spirit, just surviving by luck.”2

As Ji Yuan spoke, he focused his attention on the woman who had fallen from the locust tree. Compared to the bull demon, this woman was truly brimming with demonic energy.

The Green Vine Sword lacked fine control, so Ji Yuan wielded it himself for far less powerful or more delicate moves.

The previous strike, out of concern for Yan Fei’s proximity, had restrained the sword’s energy. But the woman was still able to sit up and stare at him with resentment, a remarkable feat.

The sword had dispelled the woman’s demonic energy just now and she had fallen to the ground. But now, a fierce, murderous aura intermingled with the demonic energy, permeating the air. The surrounding light and shadows seemed to be affected by this evil energy, making the night sky appear darker and heavier. Only her narrow eyes shone with a faint light.

At this moment, Ji Yuan’s left hand held the sword behind his back, only a corner of the hilt of the immortal sword exposed. His right hand hung empty at his side, ready to draw the sword at any time. Although he seemed calm, he was on high alert.

He was only distracted for a moment to pay attention to Yan Fei, and a piece of yellow paper floated out of his sleeve.

With the words “I await my Warrior,” an extremely sturdy warrior in golden armor appeared at Ji Yuan’s side and bowed to him.

“My lord!”

Ji Yuan glanced at the warrior out of the corner of his eye and casually ordered.

“Take care of Warrior Yan for the time being.”

“I shall obey the order!”

The Golden-Armored Warrior slowly straightened up, took a step forward, and stood in front of Yan Fei, his red face expressionless.

Ji Yuan had already prepared for a fierce battle. If the situation did not look good, he would use all of his strength to kill this demonic woman.

But things developed in a different direction at this time. Seeing Ji Yuan summon out the Golden-Armored Warrior, the demonic woman’s anger and resentment slightly sobered.

After observing Ji Yuan carefully, she noticed the immortal sword with a faint green glow behind him. The woman was stunned for a moment, then her expression changed drastically.

“The Green Vine Immortal Sword! Are you Ji Yuan!?”

“Oh!? You actually recognize me?”

Ji Yuan narrowed his eyes, sizing up this woman whose demonic aura seemed to have been greatly disturbed by these words.

At this moment, a series of bull moos and wild laughter came from behind Ji Yuan.

“Hahahahahaha… Let me take advantage and strike her down while she is weak. Thank you, Mr. Ji, for helping me! Moo—”

A flash of yellow light cut through the sky and the illusion of a giant hoof stepped down towards the woman’s location.

Boom…

The ground shook, and the place where the woman stood was swirling with dust. Ji Yuan remained motionless in the wind. The Golden-Armored Warrior did not respond aside from blocking Yan Fei from some dust and rocks.

Boom… Boom… Boom…

The giant hoof slammed down continuously, and the bull demon’s voice continued to roar.

“Let’s see whether you can survive this!”3

T/n:

dude. Why did you kidnap a woman and treat her like an object. And now you’re mad that JY “stole her from you.”

2nd phrase translation unclear, 坐好运气

Lit. “let’s see whether you live or die,” but sounded clunky in context

Chapter 300: Foolish Bull

“Ahahahahahahaha… It feels so good, so great! Die, you stinky bitch…!”1

The bull hooves descended from the sky with incredible speed. They did not trample madly, but methodically, taking into account any blind spots where the woman might escape, and stomping on the spots where the demonic energy was emanating.

Boom, boom, boom…

The roar of noise continued as dust filled the air. The large trees at the edge of the forest had long since been shattered by the blasts, and gusts of air surged outward.

Yan Fei had already finished regulating his Qi and breathing — or rather, he had finished absorbing Ji Yuan’s spiritual energy — and the influence of the demonic energy had disappeared. However, his expression was stunned, and he couldn’t suppress his shock.

The bull demon’s spiritual energy and aura were obvious to Ji Yuan, but Yan Fei could only see a halo falling from the sky, possessing truly excessive power. The earth shook almost right in front of him, and gales of wind howled, filling the sky with dust. He couldn’t even hear the arrogant laughter in the sky.

All this gave Yan Fei an indescribable shock. His let hand, still gripping the sword, trembled slightly, and his right hand was clutching at his thigh.

‘Martial arts…what use are they in the face of such a demon?’

He looked up again at the imposing figure of the Golden-Armored Warrior nearby, shrouded in a faint glow. In daylight, this light would be invisible to inattentive individuals or those with poor eyesight, but at night, it perfectly revealed the warrior’s form.

“Mr. Ji, are you an immortal?”

Ji Yuan turned to look at Yan Fei, as if sensing the complexity in his heart.

“From ordinary people’s point of view, I suppose so.”

“Moo—”

The fierce moo of a bull boomed from the sky, filling Yan Fei’s ears with a buzzing noise. He couldn’t help but cover his ears and use his Qi to withstand it.

Whoosh…

Amidst the tremendous sound of something breaking through the air, the moonlight revealed a vast shadow tearing through the swirling dust clouds, splitting the debris into crashing waves.

The bull demon, who resembled an ordinary farmer, now had a red glow in its eyes, his ferocious face bulging with flesh. It grasped a massive stone boulder, over ten zhang long, and smashed it down from the sky.

The earth energy of the surrounding ground was all flowing, converging towards the impact point as the pillar of stone fell.

Boom…

The moment the first layer of soil touched the sone, it suddenly cracked like a solid slab, then the next moment, the two ends of the “slab” of earth tilted up at both ends, and exploded into pieces.

Boom…

Huge clods of solid earth flew in all directions, several towards where Ji Yuan and Yan Fei were standing.

They were not too far away from where the bull demon had struck, and the thick smoke and dust that filled the air was rapidly approaching. As the stone erupted, Yan Fei had no time to react. However, Ji Yuan remained motionless, for there was no need for him to act.

The Golden Armored Warrior simply took a step forward to the left and swung his arms a few times.

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

“Bang!”

The left and right arms and the chest of the Golden-Armored Warrior were all covered in dust as the projectiles exploded, but the warrior did not retreat a single step, and remained calm.

“Are you dead? Are you dead now? The demonic energy is gone…”

The bull demon stood at the spot where the pillar of stone had fallen, his eyes nervously scanning the ground around his feet and his surroundings.

“No need to look for it, you have already shattered it.”

Ji Yuan’s voice sounded. The bull demon was stunned for a moment, and then a look of ecstasy appeared on his face.

“Shattered? Dead? Really dead!? Hahaha….hahahahaha…this stinky bitch is finally dead! Hahahaha…uh…”

In the midst of his wild laughter, the bull demon touched the back of his neck, and his laughter stopped abruptly: he could still feel that tuft of hair. He looked at Ji Yuan with wide eyes.

“Why is the hair still…”

Ji Yuan glanced at him without a word. He waved his arms to the left and right, sweeping away the remaining dust and debris, and strode to the collapsed crater where the ground had cracked, followed by the Golden-Armored Warrior and Yan Fei.

Ji Yuan pointed at the rubble in the crater, and the Golden-Armored Warrior stepped forward and moved the debris away one by one, revealing a large mass of brown hair underneath.

There was an indistinct, human-sized puppet wrapped in the hair, which was now in pieces. Even more bizarrely, there was blood seeping out of it continuously.

When the bull demon approached and saw this scene, he was stunned.

“She ran away!?”

“The demon escaped with a strange technique, but she didn’t escape unscathed. she must have been seriously injured by you.”

Ji Yuan frowned and looked at the puppet on the ground. It had obviously been a true demon’s body just now.

This kind of technique was somewhat familiar to Ji Yuan, but it was very different from the method he remembered. And looking at the miserable sight of the puppet oozing blood, it seemed that the other party had paid a high price…

Ji Yuan turned to look at the bull demon.

“Do you know who this woman is?”

“Uh, sir, are you referring to that dead one in the ground,2 or that stinky bitch?”

“What do you think?”

The bull demon scratched his head and said:

“I have no idea where this bitch came from. I only know that she’s incredibly evil. This Old Bull was careless and fell victim to her. She broke my self-enlightened demonic body, severely damaged my cultivation, and nearly finished me off!”

The bull demon still had lingering fears when he said these words. He had obviously been in real danger.

“A moment of carelessness? You must have been possessed by a lustful spirit!”

Ji Yuan teased. He had only known the Bull Spirit for a short period of time, but he could already see through his hobbies.

“Uh… I, I am not… Everyone has their own hobbies!”

At this time, in a small cave a few mountains away, the ground suddenly lit up in an array of light, and a pale woman emerged, her aura extremely unstable.

“Haaah… haah… haah…”

‘Thank goodness I was more careful. I have to thank that old cow. Without him, Ji Yuan might have caught me in moments, and I wouldn’t have been able to escape…’

As far as she knew, Ji Yuan’s words could be used to summon the Tao, allowing him to perform spirit-binding techniques at will. If he could see though her soul-replacing technique, with his unfathomable skills, he might be able to extract her spirit and forcibly imprison it within the wooden puppet. Even if her body escaped, it would be nothing more than a walking corpse.

Furthermore, Ji Yuan probably possessed an even more extraordinary technique, one that required no restrictions, restraints, barriers, curses, or physical contact, yet could immobilize a person’s body and spirit. That would be even more dangerous.3

“Haah… What a close call! I didn’t expect to run into Ji Yuan. I can’t stay here any longer!”

After the woman calmed herself down, she immediately set up a faint demonic wind to blow through the cave. She flew out and headed east.

. . .

Still at the grounds of their previous battle, Yan Fei stood beside Ji Yuan, looking at this man who looked like a simple farmer in fear. He wondered to himself whether this was a human or a monster, but fortunately, this being seemed very familiar with Mr. Ji.

“Mr. Ji, this stinky bitch didn’t even dare to move when she saw you. Do you know her?”

The bull demon asked in a very familiar manner.

“She should have something to do with a certain vixen. She’s not that simple!”

After Ji Yuan finished speaking, he collected a strand of hair from the ground, opened his mouth, and spit out a breath of reddish-gray air, which swept over the corpse. The brown hairs lit up with sparks, and in moments, they had turned to ash alongside the wooden puppet.

After he had finished, Ji Yuan looked at the bull demon again.

“You know me too?”

The bull demon smiled and shook his head.

“Haha, this Old Bull has never even heard of your name, Sir, but since this stinky bitch was so afraid of you, you must be a powerful immortal cultivator, right? I beg you, help me, Master, she wouldn’t even dare to fight back. It would be so satisfying!”

This answer left Ji Yuan stunned for a moment. It seemed that this bull demon, though seemingly innocent, was actually quite cunning.

“By the way, Immortal, can you help me get rid of the hair on the back of my neck? Without it, my body and spiritual energy will recover, and that stinky bitch would be no match for me.”

The bull demon rubbed his hands together, and begged in a negotiating tone.

“Just call me Mr. Ji as before. I can try, but I cannot guarantee that it will work.”

“Then what are you waiting for? What do you want me to do?”

The old bull seemed impatient. The evil hair had taken root in the nape of his neck, and was impossible to eradicate. If it weren’t for his considerable cultivation, it would have long since infiltrated his spiritual core. Even now, it was constantly draining his spiritual energy and vitality, hindering his recovery. It was truly insidious.

Ji Yuan looked at him and said:

“Dissipate your protective demonic aura, lower your head, and expose the back of your neck.”

The old bull hesitated, not daring to agree. It sounded like he was stretching his head out for execution, particularly after he had clearly seen Ji Yuan carrying a sword. That stinky woman had called it the “Green Vine Immortal Sword.”

Ji Yuan smiled.

“You are a demon, and I am an immortal cultivator. It is natural for you not to accept being defenseless against me. I can also tell you that the method I want to try is that breath I used just now.”

The reddish-gray aura had just reduced the brown hair and woman’s corpse to ash. The bull demon had seen it all, but before he could ask, Ji Yuan explained.

“I possess a True Fire technique called the True Fire of Samadhi. That aura is simply the energy of a True Fire. If I were to take advantage and catch you off guard, I could deliver a burst of True Fire…”

At this point, Ji Yuan opened his mouth and exhaled a stream of reddish-gray aura. Like a gentle breeze, it swept beneath a rock a little further away. There lay the two intact corpse puppets, pinned down by the fallen boulder.

Wherever the breath passed, the corpse puppets turned to to ash. It then swept over the other couple of headless corpses nearby, turning them to ash as well.

“Uh, hahaha… forget it, forget it, this hair has been growing for so long, its presence has become sentimental, hahaha…”

The bull demon was a little scared. Although he hadn’t seen Mr. Ji take action, he knew from that woman’s reaction earlier that he was not someone to be trifled with.

婆娘 (póniáng): derogatory slur for woman

i.e. the one the bull was carrying on his shoulders

T/n: reminder that he doesn’t exactly have this. He just dupes everyone into thinking he does by writing Edicts using ridiculous stuff like water and air. Which, I mean, frankly, close enough
